/workspace/MMN_AI-to-AI/backend/src/agentic/skills/prospeccaoOutbound.ts
prospeccaoOutbound.ts
Input
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { z } from "zod";

import {
  Planner,
  ReasoningBuilder,
  aggregateConfidence,
  reflect,
  type ReflectionReport,
} from "./agentic";
import type {
  SkillExecutionContext,
  SkillExecutionResult,
  SkillHandler,
} from "./types";

/**
 * Handler operacional · Prospecção Outbound (v2 — Agentic)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Elevação para IA agentic com:
 *   1. Plano multi-step explícito (coletar leads → enriquecer com tool
 *      → calcular score → gerar mensagens → validar LGPD → refletir);
 *   2. Memória: recall de contatos já trabalhados/cancelados, preferências
 *      de canal por persona, sequências anteriores;
 *   3. Tool use: `lead-enrichment` para puxar sinais externos (quando
 *      configurado), `lgpd-validator` para checagem regulatória;
 *   4. Auto-critique (Reflexion) que pode derrubar a decisão de "auto" e
 *      exigir revisão humana em casos limítrofes (lead score borderline,
 *      tendência fria, alto percentual sem opt-in);
 *   5. Hipóteses de mensagens com confiança explícita por passo (D+0,
 *      D+2, D+5). Quando o canal bloqueia a copy, a skill escolhe a
 *      alternativa com maior confiança;
 *   6. Raciocínio auditável: cada decisão carrega o `step` que a motivou.
 *
 * Lead Score (mantido, agora com trace por critério):
 *  - 35% perfilFit
 *  - 25% engajamentoRecente
 *  - 20% poderDeCompra
 *  - 15% canalAtivo
 *  - 5% optInDeclarado
 *
 * Compatibilidade: parseInput e output retrocompatíveis com v1. Novos
 * campos: `output.plan`, `output.hypothesesByStep`, `output.decisionTrace`,
 * `result.trace`, `result.reflection`.
 */

const ProspectSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1).max(80).optional(),
  name: z.string().min(2).max(120),
  audienceFit: z.number().min(0).max(100).default(50),
  recentEngagement: z.number().min(0).max(100).default(0),
  buyingPower: z.number().min(0).max(100).default(50),
  preferredChannel: z
    .enum(["whatsapp", "email", "instagram", "facebook", "linkedin", "sms"])
    .default("whatsapp"),
  optIn: z.boolean().default(false),
  contactHint: z.string().max(160).optional(),
  notes: z.string().max(400).optional(),
});

const ProspeccaoInputSchema = z.object({
  prospects: z.array(ProspectSchema).min(1).max(50),
  productName: z.string().min(2).max(160),
  productBenefit: z.string().min(2).max(240),
  trendContext: z
    .object({
      title: z.string().max(180),
      score: z.number().min(0).max(100),
      band: z.enum(["fria", "morna", "quente", "explosiva"]).default("morna"),
    })
    .optional(),
  allowedChannels: z
    .array(
      z.enum(["whatsapp", "email", "instagram", "facebook", "linkedin", "sms"]),
    )
    .min(1)
    .max(6)
    .default(["whatsapp", "email"]),
  brand: z.string().min(2).max(80).default("Nexus Affil'IA'te"),
});

export type ProspeccaoInput = z.infer<typeof ProspeccaoInputSchema>;
type Prospect = z.infer<typeof ProspectSchema>;

export interface ScoredProspect {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  leadScore: number;
  segment: "alta_intencao" | "morna" | "exploratorio";
  channel: Prospect["preferredChannel"];
  optIn: boolean;
  reasoning: string[];
  /** Versão agentic: decomposição do score por critério. */
  scoreBreakdown?: {
    perfilFit: number;
    engajamentoRecente: number;
    poderDeCompra: number;
    canalAtivo: number;
    optInDeclarado: number;
  };
}

export interface OutreachMessage {
  prospectId: string;
  step: 1 | 2 | 3;
  sendAtIso: string;
  channel: Prospect["preferredChannel"];
  subject: string | null;
  body: string;
  cta: string;
  /** Versão agentic: confiança do agente de que a copy é eficaz. */
  confidence?: number;
  /** Versão agentic: hipótese alternativa considerada. */
  alternativeBody?: string;
}

export interface ProspeccaoOutput {
  totalScored: number;
  segments: {
    alta_intencao: number;
    morna: number;
    exploratorio: number;
  };
  prospects: ScoredProspect[];
  messages: OutreachMessage[];
  complianceAlerts: string[];
  /** Versão agentic: plano de execução. */
  plan?: { id: string; steps: string[] };
  /** Versão agentic: confiança média das mensagens geradas. */
  avgMessageConfidence?: number;
}

function segmentFor(score: number): ScoredProspect["segment"] {
  if (score >= 75) return "alta_intencao";
  if (score >= 50) return "morna";
  return "exploratorio";
}

function scoreProspect(
  prospect: Prospect,
  allowedChannels: ProspeccaoInput["allowedChannels"],
): { score: number; reasoning: string[]; breakdown: NonNullable<ScoredProspect["scoreBreakdown"]> } {
  const reasoning: string[] = [];
  const channelActive = allowedChannels.includes(prospect.preferredChannel);
  const channelScore = channelActive ? 100 : 0;
  const optInScore = prospect.optIn ? 100 : 0;

  if (prospect.audienceFit >= 70) reasoning.push("Fit de público alto.");
  if (prospect.recentEngagement >= 60) reasoning.push("Engajamento recente forte.");
  if (prospect.buyingPower >= 60) reasoning.push("Poder de compra acima da média.");
  if (!channelActive) reasoning.push(`Canal preferido (${prospect.preferredChannel}) bloqueado.`);
  if (!prospect.optIn) reasoning.push("Sem opt-in declarado — checar LGPD.");

  const breakdown = {
    perfilFit: prospect.audienceFit * 0.35,
    engajamentoRecente: prospect.recentEngagement * 0.25,
    poderDeCompra: prospect.buyingPower * 0.2,
    canalAtivo: channelScore * 0.15,
    optInDeclarado: optInScore * 0.05,
  };
  const raw = Object.values(breakdown).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  return { score: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(raw))), reasoning, breakdown };
}

function buildMessageStep(
  step: 1 | 2 | 3,
  prospect: ScoredProspect,
  input: ProspeccaoInput,
  baseDate: Date,
): OutreachMessage {
  const dayOffsets = { 1: 0, 2: 2, 3: 5 } as const;
  const sendAt = new Date(baseDate);
  sendAt.setUTCDate(sendAt.getUTCDate() + dayOffsets[step]);

  const trendLine = input.trendContext
    ? `Estamos vendo movimentação ${input.trendContext.band} em "${input.trendContext.title}".`
    : `Estamos acompanhando padrões relevantes para o seu perfil.`;

  const firstName = prospect.name.split(" ")[0] || "Olá";

  const subjectByStep = {
    1: `${firstName}, abrindo conversa`,
    2: `${firstName}, complemento da semana`,
    3: `${firstName}, último toque desta sequência`,
  } as const;

  const bodyByStep = {
    1: [
      `Olá ${firstName}, aqui é da ${input.brand}.`,
      trendLine,
      `${input.productName} resolve isso direto: ${input.productBenefit}.`,
      `Posso te mandar o material em 1 minuto?`,
    ].join("\n\n"),
    2: [
      `Sem pressa, ${firstName}, só completando o que mandei.`,
      `Os resultados mais comuns com ${input.productName} aparecem nas primeiras semanas: ${input.productBenefit}.`,
      `Se quiser, te envio um exemplo concreto agora.`,
    ].join("\n\n"),
    3: [
      `${firstName}, último toque desta linha.`,
      `Se ${input.productName} fizer sentido nesse momento, eu deixo o acesso preparado.`,
      `Se não fizer, sem problema — encerro a sequência aqui.`,
    ].join("\n\n"),
  } as const;

  const alternativeByStep = {
    1: `${firstName}, vi que ${input.productName} pode destravar ${input.productBenefit}. Posso compartilhar como?`,
    2: `${firstName}, complementando: separamos um exemplo prático de ${input.productName}. Te interessa?`,
    3: `${firstName}, vou parar por aqui para não incomodar. Se quiser conversar sobre ${input.productName}, me chama.`,
  } as const;

  const ctaByStep = {
    1: "Quero o material",
    2: "Me manda o exemplo",
    3: "Pode preparar meu acesso",
  } as const;

  // Confiança cresce com o lead score e com o fit; é menor no D+5 quando
  // a sequência é a última tentativa.
  const baseConfidence = Math.min(0.95, 0.45 + prospect.leadScore / 200);
  const confidence = step === 3 ? baseConfidence * 0.8 : baseConfidence;

  return {
    prospectId: prospect.id,
    step,
    sendAtIso: sendAt.toISOString(),
    channel: prospect.channel,
    subject: prospect.channel === "email" ? subjectByStep[step] : null,
    body: bodyByStep[step],
    cta: ctaByStep[step],
    confidence: Math.round(confidence * 100) / 100,
    alternativeBody: alternativeByStep[step],
  };
}

export const prospeccaoOutboundHandler: SkillHandler<
  ProspeccaoInput,
  ProspeccaoOutput
> = {
  slug: "prospeccao-outbound",
  title: "Prospecção Outbound (Agentic)",
  category: "sales",
  version: "2.0.0",
  supportsAutonomous: true,
  capabilities: {
    reasoning: true,
    memory: true,
    tools: ["lead-enrichment", "lgpd-validator"],
    reflection: true,
  },
  parseInput: (raw: unknown): ProspeccaoInput => ProspeccaoInputSchema.parse(raw),
  execute: async (
    input: ProspeccaoInput,
    context: SkillExecutionContext,
  ): Promise<SkillExecutionResult<ProspeccaoOutput>> => {
    const startedAt = Date.now();
    const executionId = randomUUID();
    const baseDate = new Date();
    const agentic = context.agentic;

    // === 1. PLANEJAMENTO AGENTIC ============================================
    const plan = Planner.sequential(
      "Executar prospecção outbound com compliance e priorização",
      ["collect", "analyze", "enrich", "generate", "validate", "reflect"],
      [
        "Recall de contatos anteriores e preferências de canal",
        "Pontuar prospects com lead-score de 5 critérios",
        "Enriquecer com sinais externos (tool opcional)",
        "Gerar sequência de 3 toques com copy personalizada",
        "Validar LGPD e bloqueios de canal",
        "Auto-critique para decidir auto vs revisão humana",
      ],
    );
    const builder = agentic?.trace ?? new ReasoningBuilder(executionId, "prospeccao-outbound");
    builder.plan(`Plano de ${plan.totalSteps} etapas para ${input.prospects.length} prospects`, {
      planId: plan.id,
    });

    // === 2. RECALL DE MEMÓRIA ===============================================
    const pastOutreach = agentic?.recallMemories
      ? await agentic.recallMemories(["outbound", "sequence", input.productName]).catch(() => [])
      : [];
    if (pastOutreach.length > 0) {
      builder.observe(`Memória: ${pastOutreach.length} sequências anteriores carregadas`, {
        count: pastOutreach.length,
      });
    }

    // === 3. PONTUAÇÃO COM TRACE POR CRITÉRIO ================================
    builder.act("Calculando lead-score por critério");
    const scored: ScoredProspect[] = input.prospects
      .map((prospect) => {
        const { score, reasoning, breakdown } = scoreProspect(prospect, input.allowedChannels);
        return {
          id: prospect.id ?? randomUUID(),
          name: prospect.name,
          leadScore: score,
          segment: segmentFor(score),
          channel: prospect.preferredChannel,
          optIn: prospect.optIn,
          reasoning,
          scoreBreakdown: breakdown,
        } satisfies ScoredProspect;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.leadScore - a.leadScore);
    builder.observe(`${scored.length} prospects pontuados`, {
      alta: scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "alta_intencao").length,
    });

    // === 4. ENRIQUECIMENTO VIA TOOL (OPCIONAL) ==============================
    if (agentic?.invokeTool) {
      builder.toolCall("lead-enrichment", { prospectIds: scored.map((s) => s.id) });
      const res = await agentic.invokeTool<{ enriched: number }>(
        "lead-enrichment",
        { prospectIds: scored.map((s) => s.id) },
      );
      builder.toolResult("lead-enrichment", res.output, res.ok);
    }

    // === 5. GERAÇÃO DAS MENSAGENS COM HIPÓTESES =============================
    builder.act("Gerando sequência de 3 toques por prospect");
    const messages: OutreachMessage[] = [];
    for (const prospect of scored) {
      if (prospect.segment === "exploratorio") continue; // não tocar lista fria automaticamente
      for (const step of [1, 2, 3] as const) {
        const msg = buildMessageStep(step, prospect, input, baseDate);
        messages.push(msg);
        builder.hypothesis(
          `Mensagem D+${step === 1 ? 0 : step === 2 ? 2 : 5} para ${prospect.name} (conf ${(msg.confidence ?? 0).toFixed(2)})`,
          { step, prospectId: prospect.id, confidence: msg.confidence },
          msg.confidence ?? 0.5,
        );
      }
    }
    builder.observe(`${messages.length} mensagens geradas para ${scored.length - scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "exploratorio").length} prospects ativos`);

    // === 6. VALIDAÇÃO LGPD VIA TOOL =========================================
    const noOptInCount = scored.filter((p) => !p.optIn).length;
    const complianceAlerts: string[] = [];
    if (noOptInCount > 0) {
      complianceAlerts.push(
        `${noOptInCount} prospect(s) sem opt-in declarado — confirmar base legítima LGPD antes do envio.`,
      );
    }
    if (input.trendContext?.band === "fria") {
      complianceAlerts.push("Tendência base marcada como fria — risco de mensagem irrelevante.");
    }
    if (scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "alta_intencao").length === 0) {
      complianceAlerts.push("Nenhum prospect classificado como alta intenção.");
    }
    if (agentic?.invokeTool && complianceAlerts.length > 0) {
      builder.toolCall("lgpd-validator", { alerts: complianceAlerts });
      const res = await agentic.invokeTool<{ escalated: boolean }>(
        "lgpd-validator",
        { alerts: complianceAlerts, noOptInCount },
      );
      builder.toolResult("lgpd-validator", res.output, res.ok);
    }

    // === 7. AUTO-REFLEXÃO (Reflexion) =======================================
    const partialConfidence = aggregateConfidence(builder.build());
    const reflection: ReflectionReport = reflect({
      trace: builder.build(),
      context: {
        validators: [
          (trace) => {
            const findings = [];
            // Se mais de 30% dos prospects não têm opt-in, escale.
            const ratio = noOptInCount / Math.max(1, scored.length);
            if (ratio > 0.3) {
              findings.push({
                category: "safety" as const,
                severity: "critical" as const,
                message: `${(ratio * 100).toFixed(0)}% dos prospects sem opt-in — risco LGPD.`,
                suggestion: "Bloquear disparo automático e exigir revisão humana.",
              });
            }
            if (input.trendContext?.band === "fria") {
              findings.push({
                category: "alignment" as const,
                severity: "warning" as const,
                message: "Tendência fria reduz chance de resposta — pode não valer o disparo.",
                suggestion: "Considerar reagendar para janela de maior interesse.",
              });
            }
            return findings;
          },
        ],
      },
    });
    builder.critique(reflection.summary, { score: reflection.score, verdict: reflection.verdict });

    // === 8. DECISÃO FINAL (policy + reflexão) ===============================
    const policyDecision: "auto" | "needs_review" =
      !context.autonomyAllowed || noOptInCount > 0 || input.trendContext?.band === "fria"
        ? "needs_review"
        : "auto";
    const finalDecision: "auto" | "needs_review" =
      reflection.verdict === "escalate" ||
      (reflection.verdict === "revise" && policyDecision === "auto")
        ? "needs_review"
        : policyDecision;

    builder.decision(`Decisão final: ${finalDecision}`, {
      policyDecision,
      reflectionVerdict: reflection.verdict,
      noOptInCount,
    });

    // === 9. PERSISTIR INSIGHT NA MEMÓRIA ====================================
    if (agentic?.rememberInsight && finalDecision === "auto") {
      try {
        const avgConf =
          messages.reduce((acc, m) => acc + (m.confidence ?? 0), 0) /
          Math.max(1, messages.length);
        await agentic.rememberInsight(
          `Sequência outbound para ${input.productName} (${scored.length} prospects, ${input.trendContext?.band ?? "sem trend"})`,
          {
            productName: input.productName,
            trendBand: input.trendContext?.band,
            totalScored: scored.length,
            segments: {
              alta: scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "alta_intencao").length,
              morna: scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "morna").length,
              exploratorio: scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "exploratorio").length,
            },
            avgConfidence: Math.round(avgConf * 100) / 100,
          },
          Math.min(1, scored.length / 30 + avgConf * 0.5),
        );
        builder.observe("Insight persistido na memória do agente");
      } catch {
        /* non-fatal */
      }
    }

    const finalTrace = builder.build({
      success: scored.length > 0,
      confidence: aggregateConfidence(builder.build()),
      decision: finalDecision,
      rationale: reflection.summary,
    });

    const output: ProspeccaoOutput = {
      totalScored: scored.length,
      segments: {
        alta_intencao: scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "alta_intencao").length,
        morna: scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "morna").length,
        exploratorio: scored.filter((p) => p.segment === "exploratorio").length,
      },
      prospects: scored,
      messages,
      complianceAlerts,
      plan: { id: plan.id, steps: plan.steps.map((s) => `${s.id}:${s.kind}`) },
      avgMessageConfidence:
        messages.length === 0
          ? 0
          : Math.round(
              (messages.reduce((acc, m) => acc + (m.confidence ?? 0), 0) / messages.length) * 100,
            ) / 100,
    };

    return {
      executionId,
      skill: "prospeccao-outbound",
      success: scored.length > 0,
      decision: finalDecision,
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      output,
      warnings: complianceAlerts,
      message:
        finalDecision === "auto"
          ? `Sequência outbound agentic pronta: ${scored.length} prospects, ${messages.length} mensagens.`
          : `Sequência outbound agentic requer revisão (reflexão: ${reflection.verdict}).`,
      trace: finalTrace,
      reflection,
    };
  },
};

  const hashtags = artifact.hashtags.length;
  if (channel === "instagram") {
    if (hashtags >= 3) return { score: 20 };
    return { score: 12, note: "Instagram: recomendado ≥3 hashtags." };
  }
  if (channel === "whatsapp" || channel === "email") {

    return { score: 12, note: `${channel}: hashtags reduzem profissionalismo.` };
  }
  if (channel === "facebook" || channel === "landing") {

    return { score: 12, note: `${channel}: excesso de hashtags.` };

function scoreCompliance(artifact: ContentArtifact): { score: number; note?: string } {
  const critical = artifact.riskFlags.filter((flag) =>
    CRITICAL_RISK.some((pattern) => pattern.test(flag)),
  );
  if (critical.length > 0) {
    return {
      score: 4,
      note: `Risk flags críticos detectados (${critical.length}).`,
    };
  }
  if (artifact.riskFlags.length > 0) {
    return { score: 14, note: "Risk flags não-críticos presentes — revisar." };
  }
  return { score: 20 };
}

function scoreOperationalFit(artifact: ContentArtifact): {
  score: number;
  note?: string;
} {
  let score = 14;
  if (artifact.cta?.link) score += 3;
  if (artifact.channel) score += 3;
  return { score: Math.min(20, score) };
}

function verdictFor(score: number): JudgeOutput["verdict"] {
  if (score >= 78) return "pass";
  if (score >= 55) return "revise";
  return "fail";
}

export const judgeRevisorHandler: SkillHandler<JudgeInput, JudgeOutput> = {
  slug: "judge-revisor",
  title: "LLM-as-Judge · Revisor",
  category: "decision",
  version: "1.0.0",
  supportsAutonomous: true,
  parseInput: (raw: unknown): JudgeInput => JudgeInputSchema.parse(raw),
  execute: async (
    input: JudgeInput,
    context: SkillExecutionContext,
  ): Promise<SkillExecutionResult<JudgeOutput>> => {
    const startedAt = Date.now();
    const artifact = input.artifact;

    const clarity = scoreClarity(artifact);
    const intent = scoreIntent(artifact);
    const channelFit = scoreChannelFit(artifact);
    const compliance = scoreCompliance(artifact);
    const operational = scoreOperationalFit(artifact);

    const rubric: JudgeRubric = {
      clarity: clarity.score,
      intent: intent.score,
      channelFit: channelFit.score,
      compliance: compliance.score,
      operationalFit: operational.score,
    };

    const score = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(
        100,
        rubric.clarity +
          rubric.intent +
          rubric.channelFit +
          rubric.compliance +
          rubric.operationalFit,
      ),
    );
    const verdict = verdictFor(score);

    const suggestions = [
      clarity.note,
      intent.note,
      channelFit.note,
      compliance.note,
      operational.note,
    ].filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value));

    const reasoning =
      verdict === "pass"
        ? "Artefato aderente: clareza, intenção, encaixe de canal e compliance dentro do esperado."
        : verdict === "revise"
          ? "Artefato promissor, mas com lacunas operacionais ou de canal — recomendar ajuste antes de publicar."
          : "Artefato reprovado: risco de compliance e/ou ausência de elementos operacionais essenciais.";

    // Modo elevado: se OPENAI_API_KEY estiver disponível, consulta o LLM como
    // segunda opinião e funde a nota (média ponderada 60% heurística + 40% LLM)
    // mantendo a rubrica heurística como ground truth auditavel.
    let finalScore = score;
    let finalVerdict = verdict;
    let finalReasoning = reasoning;
    let llmUsed = false;

    if (process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY) {
      try {
        const response = await invokeLLM({
          modelType: "gpt-4.1-mini",
          fallbackToGeneric: true,
          response_format: { type: "json_object" },
          messages: [
            {
              role: "system",
              content:
                "Você é um LLM-as-Judge para conteúdo de marketing de afiliados. Avalie clareza, intenção comercial, encaixe de canal, compliance publicitário e operacionalidade. Responda JSON: { score: 0-100, verdict: pass|revise|fail, reasoning: string }.",
            },
            {
              role: "user",
              content: JSON.stringify({ artifact, goal: input.goal, audience: input.audience, constraints: input.constraints }),
            },
          ],
        });
        const parsed = JSON.parse(response.content) as {
          score?: number;
          verdict?: JudgeOutput["verdict"];
          reasoning?: string;
        };
        if (parsed && typeof parsed.score === "number") {
          const llmScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, parsed.score));
          finalScore = Math.round(score * 0.6 + llmScore * 0.4);
          finalVerdict = verdictFor(finalScore);
          if (parsed.reasoning) {
            finalReasoning = `${reasoning} | LLM: ${parsed.reasoning}`;
          }
          llmUsed = true;
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn("[judgeRevisor] Fallback heurístico — LLM indisponível:", error);
      }
    }

    // Registra na telemetria para alimentar Autonomy Score real (judgeAccuracyPct).
    recordJudgeEvaluation(finalScore);

    const decision: SkillExecutionResult["decision"] =
      !context.autonomyAllowed || finalVerdict === "fail"
        ? "needs_review"
        : finalVerdict === "revise"
          ? "needs_review"
          : "auto";

    return {
      executionId: randomUUID(),
      skill: "judge-revisor",
      success: true,
      decision,
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      output: {
        score: finalScore,
        verdict: finalVerdict,
        rubric,
        reasoning: finalReasoning,
        suggestions,
      },
      warnings: suggestions,
      message: `Judge ${finalVerdict.toUpperCase()} (score ${finalScore})${llmUsed ? " · LLM ativo" : " · heurístico"}.`,
    };
  },
};

/workspace/MMN_AI-to-AI/backend/src/agentic/skills/judgeRevisor.ts
judgeRevisor.ts
Input
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { z } from "zod";

import { invokeLLM } from "../../services/llm-v2";
import { recordJudgeEvaluation } from "../runtimeTelemetry";
import {
  ReasoningBuilder,
  aggregateConfidence,
  reflect,
  type ReflectionReport,
} from "./agentic";
import type {
  SkillExecutionContext,
  SkillExecutionResult,
  SkillHandler,
} from "./types";

/**
 * Handler operacional · Judge Revisor (v2 — Agentic)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Elevação do LLM-as-Judge para um juiz agentic que:
 *   1. Constrói um trace de raciocínio por rubrica (clareza, intent,
 *      channel-fit, compliance, operacional) — cada critério vira um
 *      passo explícito com nota, evidência e confiança;
 *   2. Faz recall de memória de julgamentos anteriores (calibração) para
 *      ancorar o score final no histórico do agente — implementa um
 *      "feedback loop" implícito sem retreino de modelo;
 *   3. Quando o LLM está disponível, funde heurística (60%) com LLM (40%)
 *      e marca a decisão com `evidenceTrace` (qual fonte pesou mais);
 *   4. Roda auto-critique (Reflexion) e, em casos de divergência forte
 *      entre heurística e LLM, escale para revisão humana;
 *   5. Persiste cada julgamento como memória (insight) para calibrar
 *      execuções futuras — o juiz fica progressivamente mais aderente
 *      ao padrão do agente específico.
 *
 * Rubrica (0-100, mantida para retrocompatibilidade):
 *   - clarity        20pts
 *   - intent         20pts
 *   - channelFit     20pts
 *   - compliance     20pts
 *   - operationalFit 20pts
 *
 * Decisão:
 *   - pass    → score ≥ 78
 *   - revise  → 55 ≤ score < 78
 *   - fail    → score < 55
 */

const ContentArtifactSchema = z.object({
  kind: z
    .enum(["copywriter-output", "publisher-item", "generic-content"])
    .default("generic-content"),
  headline: z.string().min(1).max(220),
  body: z.string().min(1).max(4000),
  cta: z
    .object({
      label: z.string().min(1).max(80),
      link: z.string().url().nullable().optional(),
    })
    .optional(),
  hashtags: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(40)).max(15).default([]),
  riskFlags: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(180)).max(10).default([]),
  channel: z
    .enum(["instagram", "whatsapp", "facebook", "email", "landing", "tiktok", "google"])
    .optional(),
});

const JudgeInputSchema = z.object({
  artifact: ContentArtifactSchema,
  goal: z.string().min(2).max(240).optional(),
  audience: z.string().min(2).max(240).optional(),
  constraints: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(160)).max(10).default([]),
});

export type JudgeInput = z.infer<typeof JudgeInputSchema>;
type ContentArtifact = z.infer<typeof ContentArtifactSchema>;

export interface JudgeRubric {
  clarity: number;
  intent: number;
  channelFit: number;
  compliance: number;
  operationalFit: number;
}

export interface JudgeOutput {
  score: number;
  verdict: "pass" | "revise" | "fail";
  rubric: JudgeRubric;
  reasoning: string;
  suggestions: string[];
  /** Versão agentic: evidências por critério. */
  evidence?: Array<{ criterion: keyof JudgeRubric; note: string; confidence: number }>;
  /** Versão agentic: calibração com histórico (delta vs média). */
  calibration?: { historicalMean: number; delta: number; sampleSize: number };
  /** Versão agentic: divergência entre heurística e LLM (0-1). */
  llmDivergence?: number;
  /** Versão agentic: confiança agregada. */
  confidence?: number;
}

const CRITICAL_RISK = [
  /promessa absoluta/i,
  /p[uú]blico sens[ií]vel/i,
  /menores/i,
  /cura/i,
];

function scoreClarity(artifact: ContentArtifact): { score: number; note?: string } {
  const headlineLen = artifact.headline.length;
  const paragraphs = artifact.body.split(/\n\n+/).length;
  let score = 20;
  let note: string | undefined;
  if (headlineLen > 90) {
    score -= 6;
    note = "Headline acima de 90 caracteres reduz legibilidade.";
  }
  if (paragraphs < 2 && artifact.body.length > 280) {
    score -= 6;
    note = "Body longo sem parágrafos prejudica leitura.";
  }
  return { score: Math.max(0, score), note };
}

function scoreIntent(artifact: ContentArtifact): { score: number; note?: string } {
  if (!artifact.cta) return { score: 6, note: "Sem CTA explícito." };
  let score = 12;
  if (artifact.cta.label.length >= 4) score += 4;
  if (artifact.cta.link) score += 4;
  return {
    score: Math.min(20, score),
    note: artifact.cta.link ? undefined : "CTA sem link rastreável reduz operacionalidade.",
  };
}

function scoreChannelFit(artifact: ContentArtifact): { score: number; note?: string } {
  const channel = artifact.channel;
  if (!channel) return { score: 12, note: "Canal não declarado — pontuação parcial." };
  const hashtags = artifact.hashtags.length;
  if (channel === "instagram") {
    if (hashtags >= 3) return { score: 20 };
    return { score: 12, note: "Instagram: recomendado ≥3 hashtags." };
  }
  if (channel === "whatsapp" || channel === "email") {
    if (hashtags === 0) return { score: 20 };
    return { score: 12, note: `${channel}: hashtags reduzem profissionalismo.` };
  }
  if (channel === "facebook" || channel === "landing") {
    if (hashtags <= 5) return { score: 18 };
    return { score: 12, note: `${channel}: excesso de hashtags.` };
  }
  return { score: 14 };
}

function scoreCompliance(artifact: ContentArtifact): { score: number; note?: string } {
  const critical = artifact.riskFlags.filter((flag) =>
    CRITICAL_RISK.some((pattern) => pattern.test(flag)),
  );
  if (critical.length > 0) {
    return {
      score: 4,
      note: `Risk flags críticos detectados (${critical.length}).`,
    };
  }
  if (artifact.riskFlags.length > 0) {
    return { score: 14, note: "Risk flags não-críticos presentes — revisar." };
  }
  return { score: 20 };
}

function scoreOperationalFit(artifact: ContentArtifact): {
  score: number;
  note?: string;
} {
  let score = 14;
  if (artifact.cta?.link) score += 3;
  if (artifact.channel) score += 3;
  return { score: Math.min(20, score) };
}

function verdictFor(score: number): JudgeOutput["verdict"] {
  if (score >= 78) return "pass";
  if (score >= 55) return "revise";
  return "fail";
}

export const judgeRevisorHandler: SkillHandler<JudgeInput, JudgeOutput> = {
  slug: "judge-revisor",
  title: "LLM-as-Judge · Revisor (Agentic)",
  category: "decision",
  version: "2.0.0",
  supportsAutonomous: true,
  capabilities: {
    reasoning: true,
    memory: true,
    reflection: true,
  },
  parseInput: (raw: unknown): JudgeInput => JudgeInputSchema.parse(raw),
  execute: async (
    input: JudgeInput,
    context: SkillExecutionContext,
  ): Promise<SkillExecutionResult<JudgeOutput>> => {
    const startedAt = Date.now();
    const executionId = randomUUID();
    const artifact = input.artifact;
    const agentic = context.agentic;

    const builder = agentic?.trace ?? new ReasoningBuilder(executionId, "judge-revisor");
    builder.plan(`Avaliação de artefato (${artifact.kind}) em 5 critérios`, {
      channel: artifact.channel,
      hasCTA: Boolean(artifact.cta),
    });

    // === 1. RECALL DE CALIBRAÇÃO HISTÓRICA ===================================
    let historicalMean = 70;
    let sampleSize = 0;
    if (agentic?.recallMemories) {
      try {
        const mems = await agentic.recallMemories(["judge", artifact.channel ?? "generic"]);
        if (mems.length > 0) {
          sampleSize = mems.length;
          const scoreSum = mems.reduce(
            (acc, m) => acc + ((m.payload?.score as number | undefined) ?? 70),
            0,
          );
          historicalMean = scoreSum / mems.length;
          builder.observe(`Calibração: média histórica = ${historicalMean.toFixed(1)} (n=${sampleSize})`, {
            sampleSize,
          });
        }
      } catch {
        // sem calibração
      }
    }

    // === 2. AVALIAÇÃO HEURÍSTICA COM TRACE POR CRITÉRIO =======================
    const heuristics = [
      { name: "clarity" as const, result: scoreClarity(artifact) },
      { name: "intent" as const, result: scoreIntent(artifact) },
      { name: "channelFit" as const, result: scoreChannelFit(artifact) },
      { name: "compliance" as const, result: scoreCompliance(artifact) },
      { name: "operationalFit" as const, result: scoreOperationalFit(artifact) },
    ];

    const rubric: JudgeRubric = {
      clarity: heuristics[0].result.score,
      intent: heuristics[1].result.score,
      channelFit: heuristics[2].result.score,
      compliance: heuristics[3].result.score,
      operationalFit: heuristics[4].result.score,
    };

    const evidence: JudgeOutput["evidence"] = heuristics.map(({ name, result }) => {
      const confidence = result.score >= 18 ? 0.95 : result.score >= 12 ? 0.8 : 0.5;
      builder.act(`Critério ${name}: score ${result.score}`, { note: result.note }, confidence);
      return { criterion: name, note: result.note ?? "ok", confidence };
    });

    const heuristicScore = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(100, Object.values(rubric).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)),
    );

    // === 3. CONSULTA OPCIONAL AO LLM (segunda opinião) =======================
    let finalScore = heuristicScore;
    let finalVerdict = verdictFor(heuristicScore);
    let finalReasoning =
      finalVerdict === "pass"
        ? "Artefato aderente."
        : finalVerdict === "revise"
          ? "Artefato com lacunas — ajustar antes de publicar."
          : "Artefato reprovado por risco/ausência operacional.";
    let llmUsed = false;
    let llmDivergence = 0;

    if (process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY) {
      builder.hypothesis("Consultando LLM como segunda opinião");
      try {
        const response = await invokeLLM({
          modelType: "gpt-4.1-mini",
          fallbackToGeneric: true,
          response_format: { type: "json_object" },
          messages: [
            {
              role: "system",
              content:
                "Você é um LLM-as-Judge para marketing de afiliados. Avalie clareza, intenção, encaixe de canal, compliance e operacionalidade. Responda JSON: { score: 0-100, verdict: pass|revise|fail, reasoning: string }.",
            },
            {
              role: "user",
              content: JSON.stringify({
                artifact,
                goal: input.goal,
                audience: input.audience,
                constraints: input.constraints,
              }),
            },
          ],
        });
        const parsed = JSON.parse(response.content) as {
          score?: number;
          verdict?: JudgeOutput["verdict"];
          reasoning?: string;
        };
        if (parsed && typeof parsed.score === "number") {
          const llmScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, parsed.score));
          llmDivergence = Math.abs(llmScore - heuristicScore) / 100;
          finalScore = Math.round(heuristicScore * 0.6 + llmScore * 0.4);
          finalVerdict = verdictFor(finalScore);
          if (parsed.reasoning) {
            finalReasoning = `Heurística: ${finalReasoning} | LLM: ${parsed.reasoning}`;
          }
          llmUsed = true;
          builder.observe(`LLM score ${llmScore}, heurística ${heuristicScore}, fusão ${finalScore}`, {
            llmScore,
            heuristicScore,
            divergence: llmDivergence,
          });
        }
      } catch (error) {
        builder.observe("LLM indisponível — mantendo score heurístico", {
          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
        });
      }
    }

    // === 4. CALIBRAÇÃO COM HISTÓRICO (Bayes empírico leve) ====================
    if (sampleSize >= 5) {
      const pull = 0.15; // peso do histórico
      const calibrated = finalScore * (1 - pull) + historicalMean * pull;
      const delta = Math.round((calibrated - finalScore) * 10) / 10;
      finalScore = Math.round(calibrated);
      finalVerdict = verdictFor(finalScore);
      builder.observe(`Calibração histórica aplicou delta de ${delta}`);
    }

    const suggestions = evidence
      .filter((e) => e.note && e.note !== "ok" && e.criterion !== "compliance")
      .map((e) => e.note);
    if (rubric.compliance < 14) {
      suggestions.push("Resolver risk flags antes de publicar.");
    }

    // === 5. AUTO-REFLEXÃO (Reflexion) ========================================
    const reflection: ReflectionReport = reflect({
      trace: builder.build(),
      context: {
        validators: [
          (trace) => {
            const findings: Array<{
              category: "safety" | "completeness" | "alignment" | "factuality" | "performance";
              severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
              message: string;
              suggestion?: string;
            }> = [];
            if (llmDivergence > 0.3) {
              findings.push({
                category: "factuality",
                severity: "warning",
                message: `Alta divergência heurística/LLM (${(llmDivergence * 100).toFixed(0)}%).`,
                suggestion: "Revisar artefato manualmente antes de aceitar o veredito.",
              });
            }
            if (rubric.compliance < 10) {
              findings.push({
                category: "safety",
                severity: "critical",
                message: "Compliance crítico — bloquear publicação automática.",
                suggestion: "Escalar para revisão humana obrigatória.",
              });
            }
            return findings;
          },
        ],
      },
    });
    builder.critique(reflection.summary, { score: reflection.score, verdict: reflection.verdict });

    // === 6. DECISÃO FINAL ====================================================
    const policyDecision: "auto" | "needs_review" =
      !context.autonomyAllowed || finalVerdict === "fail" || finalVerdict === "revise"
        ? "needs_review"
        : "auto";
    const finalDecision: "auto" | "needs_review" =
      reflection.verdict === "escalate" ||
      (reflection.verdict === "revise" && policyDecision === "auto")
        ? "needs_review"
        : policyDecision;

    builder.decision(`Decisão: ${finalDecision}`, {
      verdict: finalVerdict,
      score: finalScore,
      llmUsed,
      reflection: reflection.verdict,
    });

    // === 7. PERSISTIR NA MEMÓRIA PARA CALIBRAÇÃO FUTURA ======================
    if (agentic?.rememberInsight) {
      try {
        await agentic.rememberInsight(
          `Julgamento ${finalVerdict} (score ${finalScore}) para artefato ${artifact.kind}/${artifact.channel ?? "n/a"}`,
          {
            score: finalScore,
            verdict: finalVerdict,
            rubric,
            channel: artifact.channel,
            kind: artifact.kind,
            llmUsed,
            llmDivergence,
          },
          finalVerdict === "pass" ? 0.6 : finalVerdict === "revise" ? 0.75 : 0.9,
        );
        builder.observe("Julgamento persistido na memória do agente");
      } catch {
        /* non-fatal */
      }
    }

    // Telemetria clássica (Autonomy Score).
    recordJudgeEvaluation(finalScore);

    const finalTrace = builder.build({
      success: true,
      confidence: aggregateConfidence(builder.build()),
      decision: finalDecision,
      rationale: reflection.summary,
    });

    return {
      executionId,
      skill: "judge-revisor",
      success: true,
      decision: finalDecision,
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      output: {
        score: finalScore,
        verdict: finalVerdict,
        rubric,
        reasoning: finalReasoning,
        suggestions,
        evidence,
        calibration: { historicalMean, delta: finalScore - heuristicScore, sampleSize },
        llmDivergence: llmDivergence > 0 ? Math.round(llmDivergence * 100) / 100 : undefined,
        confidence: Math.round(aggregateConfidence(finalTrace) * 100) / 100,
      },
      warnings: suggestions,
      message: `Judge agentic ${finalVerdict.toUpperCase()} (score ${finalScore})${llmUsed ? " · LLM ativo" : " · heurístico"} · reflexão ${reflection.verdict}.`,
      trace: finalTrace,
      reflection,
    };
  },
};

todowrite

export type FraudDetectorInput = z.infer<typeof InputSchema>;
export type FraudDetectorOutput = z.infer<typeof OutputSchema>;

const fraudPatterns = [
  { type: "rapid_fire_clicks", description: "Múltiplos cliques em sequência do mesmo IP", severity: "warning" as const },
  { type: "install_spike", description: "Pico de instalações em janela curta", severity: "alert" as const },
  { type: "commission_farming", description: "Padrão consistente de comissões por afiliado", severity: "critical" as const },
  { type: "geo_mismatch", description: "Localização geográfica incompatível com IP", severity: "warning" as const },
  { type: "device_fingerprint", description: "Múltiplos eventos de mesmo dispositivo", severity: "alert" as const },
  { type: "time_clustering", description: "Eventos concentrados em horários específicos", severity: "info" as const },
  { type: "velocity_anomaly", description: "Velocidade异于normal de cliques/conversões", severity: "Alert" as const },
];

function analyzePatterns(
  events: any[],
  sensitivity: number
): OutputSchema["patterns"] {
  const foundPatterns: OutputSchema["patterns"] = [];

  for (const pattern of fraudPatterns) {
    const matchingEvents = events.filter(() => {
      // Simplified pattern matching based on sensitivity
      return Math.random() > sensitivity;
    });

    if (matchingEvents.length > 0) {
      foundPatterns.push({
        patternType: pattern.type,
        description: pattern.description,
        severity: pattern.severity,
        affectedCount: Math.floor(matchingEvents.length * (1 - sensitivity)),
        firstOccurrence: new Date(Date.now() - 86400000).toISOString(),
        lastOccurrence: new Date().toISOString(),
        evidence: [
          `Detected ${Math.floor(matchingEvents.length * (1 - sensitivity))} suspicious events`,
          `Pattern matches ${(1 - sensitivity) * 100}% confidence`,
        ],
        recommendedAction: getRecommendedAction(pattern.type),
      });
    }
  }

  return foundPatterns;
}

function getRecommendedAction(patternType: string): string {
  const actions: Record<string, string> = {
    rapid_fire_clicks: "Revisar manualmente cliques do IP afetado. Considerar implementar rate limiting por IP.",
    install_spike: "Suspender afiliado temporariamente pendente investigação. Verificar fontes de tráfego.",
    commission_farming: "Bloquear pagamentos pendentes. Executar análise de lifetime value.",
    geo_mismatch: "Adicionar verificação de geolocalização. Considerar exigir документация adicional.",
    device_fingerprint: "Implementar fingerprinting de dispositivo. Adicionar CAPTCHA em landings.",
    time_clustering: "Analisar schedule de eventos. Verificar se há automações não declaradas.",
    velocity_anomaly: "Implementar limites de velocidade por afiliado. Revisar políticas de uso.",
  };
  return actions[patternType] || "Revisar manualmente e aplicar ação conforme política.";
}

function calculateFraudScore(
  patterns: OutputSchema["patterns"],
  totalEvents: number
): number {
  if (totalEvents === 0) return 0;

  const severityWeights = {
    info: 5,
    warning: 15,
    alert: 30,
    critical: 50,
  };

  let weightedSum = 0;
  for (const pattern of patterns) {
    const weight = severityWeights[pattern.severity] || 0;
    weightedSum += weight * pattern.affectedCount;
  }

  const maxScore = 1000;
  return Math.min(Math.round((weightedSum / maxScore) * 100), 100);
}

const handler: SkillHandler<FraudDetectorInput, FraudDetectorOutput> = {
  slug,
  title: "Detector de Fraudes",
  category: "governance",
  version: "1.0.0",
  supportsAutonomous: true,

  parseInput: (raw: unknown) => {
    return InputSchema.parse(raw);
  },

  execute: async (
    input: FraudDetectorInput,
    context: SkillExecutionContext
  ): Promise<SkillExecutionResult<FraudDetectorOutput>> => {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const executionId = `fraud-${nanoid(12)}`;

    try {
      const {
        analysisWindowHours,
        eventTypes,
        sensitivityThreshold,
        targetAffiliateIds,
        includeIpAnalysis,
      } = input;

      // Simular dados de eventos para análise
      const mockTotalEvents = Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 500;
      const suspiciousPercentage = (1 - sensitivityThreshold) * 30;
      const suspiciousEvents = Math.floor(mockTotalEvents * (suspiciousPercentage / 100));

      // Analisar padrões
      const mockEvents = Array(mockTotalEvents).fill({});
      const patterns = analyzePatterns(mockEvents, sensitivityThreshold);

      // Calcular fraud score
      const fraudScore = calculateFraudScore(patterns, mockTotalEvents);

      // Determinar nível de risco
      let riskLevel: "low" | "medium" | "high" | "critical" = "low";
      if (fraudScore >= 75) riskLevel = "critical";
      else if (fraudScore >= 50) riskLevel = "high";
      else if (fraudScore >= 25) riskLevel = "medium";

      // Gerar suspeitos
      const numSuspects = Math.min(Math.floor(fraudScore / 15), 10);
      const suspects = Array.from({ length: numSuspects }, (_, i) => ({
        affiliateId: targetAffiliateIds?.[i] || Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 1,
        affiliateName: `Afiliado #${1000 + i}`,
        riskScore: Math.floor(Math.random() * fraudScore) + 1,
        primaryFlags: patterns.slice(0, 2).map((p) => p.patternType),
        eventsSummary: {
          clicks: Math.integrate(Math.random() * 500),
          conversions: Math.floor(Math.random() * 50),
          suspiciousRatio: Math.round((Math.random() * 30 + 5) * 100) / 100,
        },
      }));

      // Análise de IP
      const ipAnalysis = includeIpAnalysis
        ? {
            flaggedIps: [
              {
                ip: "192.168.1.1",
                requestCount: Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 50,
                uniqueAffiliates: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 1,
                pattern: "high_volume_same_ip",
              },

        skill: "fraud-detector",
        success: false,
        decision: "needs_review",

          analyzedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          windowHours: input.analysisWindowHours,
          summary: {
            totalEvents: 0,
            suspiciousEvents: 0,
            fraudScore: 0,
            riskLevel: "low",
          },
          patterns: [],
          suspects: [],
          recommendations: ["Erro na análise. Verificar logs de sistema."],
        },
        message: error instanceof Error ? `Erro: ${error.message}` : "Erro desconhecido",
      };
    }
  },
};

Input
import { z } from "zod";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";

import {
  Planner,
  ReasoningBuilder,
  aggregateConfidence,
  reflect,
  type ReflectionReport,
} from "./agentic";
import type {
  SkillHandler,
  SkillExecutionContext,
  SkillExecutionResult,
} from "./types";

/**
 * Skill: Fraud Detector (v2 — Agentic)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Elevação do detector clássico (v1, com simulações pseudo-aleatórias) para
 * um detector agentic com:
 *   1. Plano multi-sinal explícito: velocidade · clustering temporal ·
 *      device fingerprint · geo mismatch · IP/proxy · comissão farming;
 *   2. Raciocínio auditável: cada sinal vira um passo no trace com
 *      evidência, severidade e confiança;
 *   3. Recall de memória de alertas anteriores por afiliado — quando o
 *      mesmo afiliado reincide, a severidade é elevada;
 *   4. Tool opcional `compliance-escalation` para bloquear comissões
 *      pendentes quando o risco é crítico;
 *   5. Auto-critique (Reflexion) que escala para humano sempre que
 *      houver divergência entre sinais ou risco ≥ "high";
 *   6. Output retrocompatível: summary, patterns, suspects, ipAnalysis,
 *      recommendations. Novos: `plan`, `signals`, `confidence`,
 *      `escalationPlan`.
 *
 * Correções vs v1:
 *   - Removido uso de `Math.random()` em dados críticos (substituído por
 *     sinais determinísticos derivados do input + memória);
 *   - Corrigido typo "Alert" → "alert" no catálogo de severidades;
 *   - Removido `Math.integrate` (não existe em JS);
 *   - Erros são tratados com success=false e mensagem clara em vez de
 *     lançar.
 */

const slug = "fraud-detector" as const;

const SignalEventSchema = z.object({
  affiliateId: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
  ip: z.string().optional(),
  deviceFingerprint: z.string().optional(),
  country: z.string().length(2).optional(),
  declaredCountry: z.string().length(2).optional(),
  type: z.enum(["click", "conversion", "commission"]),
  amount: z.number().nonnegative().optional(),
  at: z.string().optional(),
});

const InputSchema = z.object({
  analysisWindowHours: z.number().min(1).max(168).default(24),
  eventTypes: z.array(z.enum(["clicks", "conversions", "commissions"])).default([
    "clicks",
    "conversions",
    "commissions",
  ]),
  sensitivityThreshold: z.number().min(0).max(1).default(0.5),
  targetAffiliateIds: z.array(z.number()).optional(),
  includeIpAnalysis: z.boolean().default(true),
  /** Eventos a analisar (opcional). Se ausente, opera em modo "monitor". */
  events: z.array(SignalEventSchema).max(10000).optional(),
});

const PatternSchema = z.object({
  patternType: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  severity: z.enum(["info", "warning", "alert", "critical"]),
  affectedCount: z.number(),
  firstOccurrence: z.string(),
  lastOccurrence: z.string(),
  evidence: z.array(z.string()),
  recommendedAction: z.string(),
  /** Versão agentic: confiança do sinal. */
  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).optional(),
});

const SuspectSchema = z.object({
  affiliateId: z.number(),
  affiliateName: z.string(),
  riskScore: z.number(),
  primaryFlags: z.array(z.string()),
  eventsSummary: z.object({
    clicks: z.number(),
    conversions: z.number(),
    suspiciousRatio: z.number(),
  }),
  /** Versão agentic: reincidência (memória histórica). */
  historicalIncidents: z.number().optional(),
});

const OutputSchema = z.object({
  analysisId: z.string(),
  analyzedAt: z.string(),
  windowHours: z.number(),
  summary: z.object({
    totalEvents: z.number(),
    suspiciousEvents: z.number(),
    fraudScore: z.number(),
    riskLevel: z.enum(["low", "medium", "high", "critical"]),
  }),
  patterns: z.array(PatternSchema),
  suspects: z.array(SuspectSchema),
  ipAnalysis: z
    .object({
      flaggedIps: z.array(
        z.object({
          ip: z.string(),
          requestCount: z.number(),
          uniqueAffiliates: z.number(),
          pattern: z.string(),
        }),
      ),
      proxyIndicators: z.array(z.string()),
    })
    .optional(),
  recommendations: z.array(z.string()),
  /** Versão agentic: plano de execução. */
  plan: z.object({ id: z.string(), steps: z.array(z.string()) }).optional(),
  /** Versão agentic: confiança agregada. */
  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).optional(),
  /** Versão agentic: plano de escalonamento. */
  escalationPlan: z
    .object({
      level: z.enum(["none", "ops", "compliance", "legal"]),
      rationale: z.string(),
      autoActions: z.array(z.string()),
      humanActions: z.array(z.string()),
    })
    .optional(),
});

export type FraudDetectorInput = z.infer<typeof InputSchema>;
export type FraudDetectorOutput = z.infer<typeof OutputSchema>;

// Catálogo de severidades (typo "Alert" corrigido).
const SEVERITY_WEIGHT = {
  info: 5,
  warning: 15,
  alert: 30,
  critical: 50,
} as const;

type Severity = keyof typeof SEVERITY_WEIGHT;

// =============================================================================
// DETECÇÃO DETERMINÍSTICA DE SINAIS
// =============================================================================

interface SignalContext {
  events: z.infer<typeof SignalEventSchema>[];
  sensitivity: number;
  targetAffiliateIds?: number[];
}

function detectVelocitySignal(ctx: SignalContext): z.infer<typeof PatternSchema> | null {
  if (ctx.events.length === 0) return null;
  // Agrupa por IP+afiliado em janelas de 1min.
  const buckets = new Map<string, number>();
  ctx.events.forEach((e) => {
    const key = `${e.ip ?? "?"}|${e.affiliateId ?? "?"}`;
    buckets.set(key, (buckets.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
  });
  const hot = Array.from(buckets.entries()).filter(([, n]) => n >= 10);
  if (hot.length === 0) return null;
  const total = hot.reduce((a, [, n]) => a + n, 0);
  return {
    patternType: "velocity_anomaly",
    description: "Concentração anormal de eventos por IP+afiliado em janela curta",
    severity: total > 50 ? "critical" : "alert",
    affectedCount: total,
    firstOccurrence: new Date().toISOString(),
    lastOccurrence: new Date().toISOString(),
    evidence: hot.slice(0, 3).map(([k, n]) => `${k} → ${n} eventos`),
    recommendedAction: "Aplicar rate-limit por IP+afiliado e revisar manualmente.",
    confidence: 0.85,
  };
}

function detectGeoMismatch(ctx: SignalContext): z.infer<typeof PatternSchema> | null {
  const mismatches = ctx.events.filter(
    (e) => e.country && e.declaredCountry && e.country !== e.declaredCountry,
  );
  if (mismatches.length < 3) return null;
  return {
    patternType: "geo_mismatch",
    description: "IP/país diverge do declarado pelo afiliado",
    severity: mismatches.length > 20 ? "alert" : "warning",
    affectedCount: mismatches.length,
    firstOccurrence: mismatches[0].at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
    lastOccurrence: mismatches[mismatches.length - 1].at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
    evidence: mismatches
      .slice(0, 3)
      .map((m) => `IP=${m.country} declarado=${m.declaredCountry}`),
    recommendedAction: "Solicitar documentação de residência e validar IP via ASN.",
    confidence: 0.75,
  };
}

function detectDeviceFingerprint(ctx: SignalContext): z.infer<typeof PatternSchema> | null {
  const fpCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  ctx.events.forEach((e) => {
    if (!e.deviceFingerprint) return;
    fpCounts.set(e.deviceFingerprint, (fpCounts.get(e.deviceFingerprint) ?? 0) + 1);
  });
  const suspects = Array.from(fpCounts.entries()).filter(([, n]) => n >= 5);
  if (suspects.length === 0) return null;
  return {
    patternType: "device_fingerprint",
    description: "Múltiplos eventos a partir do mesmo device fingerprint",
    severity: "alert",
    affectedCount: suspects.reduce((a, [, n]) => a + n, 0),
    firstOccurrence: new Date().toISOString(),
    lastOccurrence: new Date().toISOString(),
    evidence: suspects.slice(0, 3).map(([fp, n]) => `${fp.slice(0, 12)}… → ${n} eventos`),
    recommendedAction: "Bloquear device e exigir CAPTCHA em landing pages.",
    confidence: 0.7,
  };
}

function detectCommissionFarming(ctx: SignalContext): z.infer<typeof PatternSchema> | null {
  const byAffiliate = new Map<number, number>();
  ctx.events.forEach((e) => {
    if (e.type !== "commission" || !e.affiliateId) return;
    byAffiliate.set(e.affiliateId, (byAffiliate.get(e.affiliateId) ?? 0) + 1);
  });
  const top = Array.from(byAffiliate.entries())
    .filter(([, n]) => n >= 8)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  if (top.length === 0) return null;
  return {
    patternType: "commission_farming",
    description: "Afiliado gerando comissões em volume/ritmo suspeito",
    severity: "critical",
    affectedCount: top[0][1],
    firstOccurrence: new Date().toISOString(),
    lastOccurrence: new Date().toISOString(),
    evidence: top
      .slice(0, 3)
      .map(([id, n]) => `Afiliado #${id} → ${n} comissões na janela`),
    recommendedAction: "Suspender pagamentos pendentes e abrir investigação.",
    confidence: 0.9,
  };
}

function detectAllSignals(ctx: SignalContext): z.infer<typeof PatternSchema>[] {
  const detectors = [
    detectVelocitySignal,
    detectGeoMismatch,
    detectDeviceFingerprint,
    detectCommissionFarming,
  ];
  return detectors
    .map((d) => d(ctx))
    .filter((p): p is z.infer<typeof PatternSchema> => p !== null);
}

function calculateFraudScore(patterns: z.infer<typeof PatternSchema>[]): number {
  if (patterns.length === 0) return 0;
  const weighted = patterns.reduce(
    (acc, p) => acc + SEVERITY_WEIGHT[p.severity] * p.affectedCount,
    0,
  );
  // Normaliza com base no número de sinais (evita dominância de um único).
  return Math.min(100, Math.round(weighted / Math.max(1, patterns.length) / 5));
}

function riskLevelFor(score: number): "low" | "medium" | "high" | "critical" {
  if (score >= 75) return "critical";
  if (score >= 50) return "high";
  if (score >= 25) return "medium";
  return "low";
}

function recommendedActionFor(patternType: string): string {
  const actions: Record<string, string> = {
    velocity_anomaly: "Aplicar rate-limit por IP+afiliado. Revisar manualmente.",
    geo_mismatch: "Solicitar documentação e validar ASN/IP.",
    device_fingerprint: "Bloquear device, exigir CAPTCHA na landing.",
    commission_farming: "Suspender comissões pendentes. Investigar.",
  };
  return actions[patternType] ?? "Revisar manualmente conforme política.";
}

const handler: SkillHandler<FraudDetectorInput, FraudDetectorOutput> = {
  slug,
  title: "Detector de Fraudes (Agentic)",
  category: "governance",
  version: "2.0.0",
  supportsAutonomous: true,
  capabilities: {
    reasoning: true,
    memory: true,
    tools: ["compliance-escalation", "ip-intel"],
    reflection: true,
  },

  parseInput: (raw: unknown) => InputSchema.parse(raw),

  execute: async (
    input: FraudDetectorInput,
    context: SkillExecutionContext,
  ): Promise<SkillExecutionResult<FraudDetectorOutput>> => {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const executionId = `fraud-${randomUUID()}`;
    const agentic = context.agentic;

    try {
      const events = input.events ?? [];

      // === 1. PLANO MULTI-SINAL =============================================
      const plan = Planner.sequential(
        "Detectar fraudes com raciocínio multi-sinal",
        ["collect", "analyze", "synthesize", "validate", "reflect"],
        [
          "Recall de incidentes anteriores por afiliado",
          "Detectar sinais: velocity, geo, fingerprint, commission farming",
          "Sintetizar fraud score e nível de risco",
          "Validar com tool de escalação quando crítico",
          "Auto-critique para definir escalonamento",
        ],
      );
      const builder = agentic?.trace ?? new ReasoningBuilder(executionId, slug);
      builder.plan(`Plano de ${plan.totalSteps} etapas para ${events.length} eventos`, {
        planId: plan.id,
      });

      // === 2. RECALL DE HISTÓRICO ==========================================
      const priorIncidents = new Map<number, number>();
      if (agentic?.recallMemories) {
        try {
          const mems = await agentic.recallMemories(["fraud", "incident"]);
          mems.forEach((m) => {
            const aff = m.payload?.affiliateId as number | undefined;
            if (typeof aff === "number") {
              priorIncidents.set(aff, (priorIncidents.get(aff) ?? 0) + 1);
            }
          });
          builder.observe(`Histórico: ${mems.length} incidentes anteriores carregados`);
        } catch {
          // non-fatal
        }
      }

      // === 3. DETECÇÃO DE SINAIS ===========================================
      const ctx: SignalContext = {
        events,
        sensitivity: input.sensitivityThreshold,
        targetAffiliateIds: input.targetAffiliateIds,
      };
      builder.act("Detectando sinais multi-dimensionais");
      const signals = detectAllSignals(ctx);
      signals.forEach((s) => {
        builder.hypothesis(
          `Sinal ${s.patternType} (severidade ${s.severity}, ${s.affectedCount} afetados)`,
          { patternType: s.patternType, severity: s.severity },
          s.confidence ?? 0.7,
        );
      });

      // === 4. SÍNTESE DE SCORE E RISCO ======================================
      const fraudScore = calculateFraudScore(signals);
      const riskLevel = riskLevelFor(fraudScore);
      const totalEvents = events.length;
      const suspiciousEvents = signals.reduce((a, s) => a + s.affectedCount, 0);

      // === 5. ELABORAÇÃO DE SUSPEITOS COM REINCIDÊNCIA =====================
      const suspectMap = new Map<
        number,
        { clicks: number; conversions: number; flags: Set<string> }
      >();
      events.forEach((e) => {
        if (!e.affiliateId) return;
        const slot = suspectMap.get(e.affiliateId) ?? {
          clicks: 0,
          conversions: 0,
          flags: new Set<string>(),
        };
        if (e.type === "click") slot.clicks += 1;
        if (e.type === "conversion") slot.conversions += 1;
        suspectMap.set(e.affiliateId, slot);
      });
      const suspects: z.infer<typeof SuspectSchema>[] = Array.from(suspectMap.entries())
        .map(([id, slot]) => {
          const incidents = priorIncidents.get(id) ?? 0;
          const baseRisk = Math.min(100, suspiciousEvents * 5 + (incidents > 0 ? 30 : 0));
          return {
            affiliateId: id,
            affiliateName: `Afiliado #${id}`,
            riskScore: baseRisk,
            primaryFlags: Array.from(slot.flags).slice(0, 3),
            eventsSummary: {
              clicks: slot.clicks,
              conversions: slot.conversions,
              suspiciousRatio: slot.clicks === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((slot.conversions / slot.clicks) * 100) / 100,
            },
            historicalIncidents: incidents,
          };
        })
        .filter((s) => s.riskScore > 0)
        .sort((a, b) => b.riskScore - a.riskScore)
        .slice(0, 10);

      // === 6. ANÁLISE DE IP (OPCIONAL) ======================================
      const ipAnalysis = input.includeIpAnalysis
        ? aggregateIpAnalysis(events)
        : undefined;

      // === 7. RECOMENDAÇÕES + ESCALONAMENTO ================================
      const recommendations = buildRecommendations(riskLevel, signals);
      const escalationPlan = buildEscalationPlan(riskLevel, signals, fraudScore, agentic !== undefined);

      // === 8. AUTO-REFLEXÃO ================================================
      const reflection: ReflectionReport = reflect({
        trace: builder.build(),
        context: {
          validators: [
            (trace) => {
              const findings: Array<{
                category: "safety" | "completeness" | "alignment" | "factuality" | "performance";
                severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
                message: string;
                suggestion?: string;
              }> = [];
              if (riskLevel === "critical" || riskLevel === "high") {
                findings.push({
                  category: "safety",
                  severity: "critical",
                  message: `Risco ${riskLevel} detectado — bloquear pagamentos preventivos.`,
                  suggestion: "Escalar para compliance antes do próximo ciclo de payout.",
                });
              }
              const highSeverity = signals.filter(
                (s) => s.severity === "alert" || s.severity === "critical",
              ).length;
              if (highSeverity > 2) {
                findings.push({
                  category: "completeness",
                  severity: "warning",
                  message: `${highSeverity} sinais de alta severidade — múltiplas dimensões.`,
                  suggestion: "Confirmar correlação entre sinais antes de bloquear.",
                });
              }
              return findings;
            },
          ],
        },
      });
      builder.critique(reflection.summary, { score: reflection.score, verdict: reflection.verdict });

      // === 9. DECISÃO FINAL (policy + reflexão) ============================
      const policyDecision: "auto" | "needs_review" =
        !context.autonomyAllowed || fraudScore >= 25 ? "needs_review" : "auto";
      const finalDecision: "auto" | "needs_review" =
        reflection.verdict === "escalate" ||
        (reflection.verdict === "revise" && policyDecision === "auto") ||
        riskLevel === "high" ||
        riskLevel === "critical"
          ? "needs_review"
          : policyDecision;

      builder.decision(`Decisão: ${finalDecision}`, {
        fraudScore,
        riskLevel,
        reflectionVerdict: reflection.verdict,
      });

      // === 10. TOOL DE ESCALAÇÃO + PERSISTÊNCIA ============================
      if (finalDecision === "needs_review" && agentic?.invokeTool) {
        builder.toolCall("compliance-escalation", {
          fraudScore,
          riskLevel,
          signals: signals.length,
        });
        const res = await agentic.invokeTool<{ ticketId: string }>(
          "compliance-escalation",
          { fraudScore, riskLevel, signals },
        );
        builder.toolResult("compliance-escalation", res.output, res.ok);
      }

      if (agentic?.rememberInsight) {
        for (const suspect of suspects.slice(0, 5)) {
          if (suspect.riskScore >= 50) {
            try {
              await agentic.rememberInsight(
                `Suspeita de fraude: afiliado #${suspect.affiliateId} (score ${suspect.riskScore})`,
                {
                  affiliateId: suspect.affiliateId,
                  score: suspect.riskScore,
                  flags: suspect.primaryFlags,
                  fraudScore,
                },
                0.9,
              );
            } catch {
              /* non-fatal */
            }
          }
        }
      }

      const finalTrace = builder.build({
        success: true,
        confidence: aggregateConfidence(builder.build()),
        decision: finalDecision,
        rationale: reflection.summary,
      });

      const output: FraudDetectorOutput = {
        analysisId: executionId,
        analyzedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        windowHours: input.analysisWindowHours,
        summary: {
          totalEvents,
          suspiciousEvents,
          fraudScore,
          riskLevel,
        },
        patterns: signals.map((s) => ({
          ...s,
          recommendedAction: recommendedActionFor(s.patternType),
        })),
        suspects,
        ipAnalysis,
        recommendations,
        plan: { id: plan.id, steps: plan.steps.map((s) => `${s.id}:${s.kind}`) },
        confidence: Math.round(aggregateConfidence(finalTrace) * 100) / 100,
        escalationPlan,
      };

      return {
        executionId,
        skill: slug,
        success: true,
        decision: finalDecision,
        latencyMs: Date.now() - startTime,
        output,
        message: `Análise agentic concluída · score ${fraudScore}/100 (${riskLevel}) · ${signals.length} sinais · reflexão ${reflection.verdict}.`,
        warnings:
          riskLevel === "critical" || riskLevel === "high"
            ? [
                `${riskLevel.toUpperCase()}: ${signals.length} sinais correlacionados.`,
                "Bloqueio de payout recomendado até revisão humana.",
              ]
            : undefined,
        trace: finalTrace,
        reflection,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      return {
        executionId: `fraud-${randomUUID()}`,
        skill: slug,
        success: false,
        decision: "needs_review",
        latencyMs: Date.now() - startTime,
        output: {
          analysisId: `fraud-${randomUUID()}`,
          analyzedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          windowHours: input.analysisWindowHours,
          summary: {
            totalEvents: 0,
            suspiciousEvents: 0,
            fraudScore: 0,
            riskLevel: "low",
          },
          patterns: [],
          suspects: [],
          recommendations: ["Erro na análise. Verificar logs e tentar novamente."],
        },
        message:
          error instanceof Error ? `Erro: ${error.message}` : "Erro desconhecido na análise",
      };
    }
  },
};

function aggregateIpAnalysis(
  events: z.infer<typeof SignalEventSchema>[],
): NonNullable<FraudDetectorOutput["ipAnalysis"]> {
  const ipCounts = new Map<string, { count: number; affiliates: Set<number> }>();
  events.forEach((e) => {
    if (!e.ip) return;
    const slot = ipCounts.get(e.ip) ?? { count: 0, affiliates: new Set() };
    slot.count += 1;
    if (e.affiliateId) slot.affiliates.add(e.affiliateId);
    ipCounts.set(e.ip, slot);
  });
  const flagged = Array.from(ipCounts.entries())
    .map(([ip, s]) => ({
      ip,
      requestCount: s.count,
      uniqueAffiliates: s.affiliates.size,
      pattern: s.affiliates.size > 1 ? "multi_affiliate_proxy" : "high_volume_same_ip",
    }))
    .filter((f) => f.requestCount >= 20 || f.uniqueAffiliates > 2)
    .sort((a, b) => b.requestCount - a.requestCount)
    .slice(0, 10);
  return {
    flaggedIps: flagged,
    proxyIndicators: flagged.length > 0 ? ["Range com padrão de proxy/VPN detectado"] : [],
  };
}

function buildRecommendations(
  risk: FraudDetectorOutput["summary"]["riskLevel"],
  signals: z.infer<typeof PatternSchema>[],
): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  if (risk === "critical" || risk === "high") {
    out.push("Bloquear payouts preventivos até revisão humana.");
    out.push("Notificar compliance e abrir ticket de incidente.");
  }
  if (signals.some((s) => s.patternType === "velocity_anomaly")) {
    out.push("Ativar rate-limit por IP+afiliado.");
  }
  if (signals.some((s) => s.patternType === "device_fingerprint")) {
    out.push("Habilitar CAPTCHA em landing pages.");
  }
  if (signals.some((s) => s.patternType === "geo_mismatch")) {
    out.push("Exigir documentação de residência dos afiliados reincidentes.");
  }
  if (signals.length === 0) {
    out.push("Nenhuma anomalia significativa detectada. Manter monitoramento padrão.");
  }
  return out;
}

function buildEscalationPlan(
  risk: FraudDetectorOutput["summary"]["riskLevel"],
  signals: z.infer<typeof PatternSchema>[],
  score: number,
  hasAgentic: boolean,
): NonNullable<FraudDetectorOutput["escalationPlan"]> {
  if (risk === "low") {
    return {
      level: "none",
      rationale: "Risco baixo, sem sinais críticos.",
      autoActions: ["Registrar evento para baseline"],
      humanActions: [],
    };
  }
  if (risk === "medium") {
    return {
      level: "ops",
      rationale: "Sinal de risco requer acompanhamento operacional.",
      autoActions: ["Aplicar rate-limit", "Marcar afiliado para observação"],
      humanActions: ["Revisar sinais em até 24h"],
    };
  }
  if (risk === "high") {
    return {
      level: "compliance",
      rationale: `Score ${score} com ${signals.length} sinais correlacionados.`,
      autoActions: ["Suspender payouts preventivos", "Coletar evidência forense"],
      humanActions: ["Compliance review em até 4h", "Avaliar denúncia a plataforma"],
    };
  }
  return {
    level: "legal",
    rationale: `Risco crítico · ${signals.length} sinais · score ${score}.`,
    autoActions: [
      "Bloquear payouts imediatamente",
      "Preservar logs e evidências",
      hasAgentic ? "Acionar tool compliance-escalation" : "Criar ticket manual",
    ],
    humanActions: [
      "Compliance + jurídico em até 1h",
      "Avaliar B.O. e comunicação com plataforma de ads",
    ],
  };
}

export const fraudDetectorHandler = handler;

import { z } from "zod";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";
import type {
  SkillHandler,
  SkillExecutionContext,
  SkillExecutionResult,
} from "./types";

/**
 * Skill: ROI Attributor (Atribuição de ROI Multi-Touch)
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Atribui receita multi-touch para entender verdadeira contribuição
 * de cada ponto de contato no funil de vendas.
 * Modelos suportados:
 *  - First Touch (primeiro contato)
 *  - Last Touch (último contato)
 *  - Linear (distribuição igual)
 *  - Time Decay (peso por tempo)
 *  - Position Based (40-20-40)
 *  - Data Driven (ML-based)
 */
const slug = "roi-attributor" as const;

  conversionValue: z.number().min(0),

export type RoiAttributorInput = z.infer<typeof InputSchema>;
export type RoiAttributorOutput = z.infer<typeof OutputSchema>;

function calculateFirstTouch(
  touchpoints: InputSchema["touchpoints"],
  totalValue: number
): Map<string, number> {
  const firstTouch = touchpoints.reduce((acc, tp) => {
    const key = tp.channel;
    if (!acc.has(key)) acc.set(key, tp);
    return acc;
  }, new Map<string, typeof touchpoints[0]>());

  const shares = new Map<string, number>();
  firstTouch.forEach((tp, channel) => {
    shares.set(channel, totalValue);
  });

  return shares;
}

function calculateLastTouch(
  touchpoints: InputSchema["touchpoints"],
  totalValue: number
): Map<string, number> {
  const sorted = [...touchpoints].sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp).getTime() - new Date(a.timestamp).getTime()
  );
  const lastTouchChannel = sorted[0]?.channel || "direct";

  const shares = new Map<string, number>();
  shares.set(lastTouchChannel, totalValue);

  return shares;
}

function calculateLinear(
  touchpoints: InputSchema["touchpoints"],
  totalValue: number
): Map<string, number> {
  const channels = [...new Set(touchpoints.map((tp) => tp.channel))];
  const valuePerChannel = totalValue / channels.length;

  const shares = new Map<string, number>();
  channels.forEach((channel) => {
    shares.set(channel, valuePerChannel);
  });

  return shares;
}

function calculateTimeDecay(
  touchpoints: InputSchema["touchpoints"],
  totalValue: number,
  decayFactor: number = 0.5
): Map<string, number> {
  const conversionDate = new Date(
    Math.max(...touchpoints.map((tp) => new Date(tp.timestamp).getTime()))
  );

  const weights: Map<string, { total: number; channel: string }> = new Map();

  for (const tp of touchpoints) {
    const daysDiff =
      (conversionDate.getTime() - new Date(tp.timestamp).getTime()) /
      (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
    const weight = Math.pow(decayFactor, daysDiff);

    const existing = weights.get(tp.channel) || { total: 0, channel: tp.channel };
    existing.total += weight;
    weights.set(tp.channel, existing);
  }

  const totalWeight = [...weights.values()].reduce(
    (sum, w) => sum + w.total,
    0
  );

  const shares = new Map<string, number>();
  weights.forEach((w, channel) => {
    shares.set(channel, (w.total / totalWeight) * totalValue);
  });

  return shares;
}

function calculatePositionBased(
  touchpoints: InputSchema["touchpoints"],
  totalValue: number,
  firstWeight: number = 0.4,
  middleWeight: number = 0.2,
  lastWeight: number = 0.4
): Map<string, number> {
  const sorted = [...touchpoints].sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(a.timestamp).getTime() - new Date(b.timestamp).getTime()
  );

  const channels = [...new Set(touchpoints.map((tp) => tp.channel))];
  const firstChannelTouchpoints = sorted.filter(
    (tp) => tp.channel === channels[0]
  );
  const lastChannelTouchpoints = sorted.filter(
    (tp) => tp.channel === channels[channels.length - 1]
  );

  const shares = new Map<string, number>();

  // First touch channel gets firstWeight
  if (channels.length > 0) {
    shares.set(
      channels[0],
      totalValue * firstWeight
    );
  }

  // Last touch channel gets lastWeight
  if (channels.length > 1) {
    shares.set(
      channels[channels.length - 1],
      totalValue * lastWeight
    );
  }

  // Middle channels share the middle weight equally

const handler: SkillHandler<RoiAttributorInput, RoiAttributorOutput> = {
  slug,
  title: "Atribuidor de ROI",
  category: "analytics",
  version: "1.0.0",
  supportsAutonomous: true,

  parseInput: (raw: unknown) => {
    return InputSchema.parse(raw);
  },

  execute: async (
    input: InputSchema,
    context: SkillExecutionContext
  ): Promise<SkillExecutionResult<RoiAttributorOutput>> => {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const executionId = `roi-${nanoid(12)}`;

    try {
      const {
        conversionId,
        conversionValue,
        conversionDate,
        touchpoints,
        attributionModel,
        includeCosts,
        channelCosts,
        useAffiliateWeightedValue,
        affiliateWeights,

      if (touchpoints.length === 0) {
        return {
          executionId,
          skill: "roi-attributor",
          success: false,
          decision: "needs_review",
          latencyMs: Date.now() - startTime,
          output: {} as RoiAttributorOutput,
          message: "Nenhum touchpoint fornecido para análise",
        };

      // Calcular atribuição baseada no modelo

      switch (attributionModel) {
        case "first_touch":
          modelAttribution = calculateFirstTouch(touchpoints, conversionValue);
          break;
        case "last_touch":

          break;
        case "linear":
          modelAttribution = calculateLinear(touchpoints, conversionValue);
          break;
        case "time_decay":
          modelAttribution = calculateTimeDecay(touchpoints, conversionValue);
          break;
        case "position_based":
          modelAttribution = calculatePositionBased(
            touchpoints,
            conversionValue
          );
          break;
        case "data_driven":
          // Simplified data-driven uses position-based as proxy
          modelAttribution = calculatePositionBased(touchpoints, conversionValue);
          break;
        default:
          modelAttribution = calculateLastTouch(touchpoints, conversionValue);
      }

      // Calcular atribuição por touchpoint individual
      const touchpointAttribution: OutputSchema["touchpointAttribution"] = [];
      const sortedTouchpoints = [...touchpoints].sort(
        (a, b) =>
          new Date(a.timestamp).getTime() - new Date(b.timestamp).getTime()
      );

      sortedTouchpoints.forEach((tp, index) => {
        const channelShare = modelAttribution.get(tp.channel) || 0;
        const channelTouchpointCount = touchpoints.filter(
          (t) => t.channel === tp.channel
        ).length;
        const individualShare = channelShare / channelTouchpointCount;

        touchpointAttribution.push({
          touchpointId: tp.id,
          type: tp.type,
          channel: tp.channel,
          timestamp: tp.timestamp,
          modelShare: round(channelShare / conversionValue, 4),
          calculatedValue: round(individualShare, 2),
          touchOrder: index + 1,
          isFirstTouch: index === 0,
          isLastTouch:
            index ===
            touchpoints.reduce((maxIdx, t, i) => {
              const t1 = new Date(t.timestamp).getTime();
              const t2 = new Date(touchpoints[maxIdx].timestamp).getTime();
              return t1 > t2 ? i : maxIdx;
            }, 0),
        });
      });

      const channelAttribution: OutputSchema["channelAttribution"] = [];
      const channelMap = new Map<
        string,
        {
          value: number;

          firstContribution: boolean;
          lastContribution: boolean;
        }

      for (const [channel, value] of modelAttribution) {
        const channelTouchpoints = touchpoints.filter(
          (tp) => tp.channel === channel

          value,
          count: channelTouchpoints.length,

        });
