import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";

import {
  DEFAULT_AGENTIC_CONFIG,
  InMemorySkillMemory,
  Planner,
  ReasoningBuilder,
  ToolRegistry,
  aggregateConfidence,
  noArgsSchema,
  reflect,
  setDefaultSkillMemory,
  setDefaultToolRegistry,
} from "../agentic";

/**
 * Testes unitários da infraestrutura agentic compartilhada.
 * Garante que o ciclo plan → act → observe → reflect → remember
 * funciona isoladamente, sem dependência de I/O, LLM ou DB.
 */

describe("ReasoningBuilder", () => {
  it("constrói trace com steps sequenciais e timestamps", () => {
    const b = new ReasoningBuilder("exec-1", "test-skill");
    b.plan("plano inicial");
    b.act("executando");
    b.observe("observado");
    b.decision("auto");
    const trace = b.build({
      success: true,
      confidence: 0.9,
      decision: "auto",
      rationale: "ok",
    });

    expect(trace.executionId).toBe("exec-1");
    expect(trace.skill).toBe("test-skill");
    expect(trace.steps).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(trace.steps[0].kind).toBe("plan");
    expect(trace.steps[3].kind).toBe("decision");
    expect(trace.outcome?.confidence).toBe(0.9);
    expect(trace.finishedAt).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("calcula confiança agregada corretamente", () => {
    const b = new ReasoningBuilder("exec-2", "test");
    b.act("a", undefined, 1);
    b.act("b", undefined, 0.6);
    b.act("c", undefined, 0.4);
    const trace = b.build();
    const conf = aggregateConfidence(trace);
    expect(conf).toBeCloseTo((1 + 0.6 + 0.4) / 3, 5);
  });

  it("retorna 0.5 quando não há passos com confiança", () => {
    const b = new ReasoningBuilder("exec-3", "test");
    b.plan("p");
    const trace = b.build();
    expect(aggregateConfidence(trace)).toBe(0.5);
  });

  it("registra tool_call e tool_result com deltas", () => {
    const b = new ReasoningBuilder("exec-4", "test");
    b.toolCall("crm.lookup", { id: 1 });
    b.toolResult("crm.lookup", { ok: true }, true);
    const trace = b.build();
    expect(trace.steps[0].kind).toBe("tool_call");
    expect(trace.steps[1].kind).toBe("tool_result");
    expect(trace.steps[1].confidence).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("Planner", () => {
  it("constrói plano sequencial", () => {
    const plan = Planner.sequential(
      "obj",
      ["analyze", "generate", "validate"],
      ["analisar", "gerar", "validar"],
    );
    expect(plan.steps).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(plan.steps[0].dependsOn).toEqual([]);
    expect(plan.steps[1].dependsOn).toEqual(["s1"]);
    expect(plan.steps[2].dependsOn).toEqual(["s2"]);
  });

  it("rejeita kinds e descriptions de tamanhos diferentes", () => {
    expect(() =>
      Planner.sequential("o", ["analyze"], ["a", "b"]),
    ).toThrowError();
  });

  it("nextReady retorna a próxima etapa sem dependências pendentes", () => {
    const plan = Planner.fromSteps("o", [
      { kind: "analyze", description: "a" },
      { kind: "generate", description: "g", dependsOn: ["s1"] },
    ]);
    const state = {
      planId: plan.id,
      currentStepIndex: 0,
      completedSteps: [],
      failedSteps: [],
      artifacts: {},
    };
    expect(Planner.nextReady(plan, state)?.id).toBe("s1");
    state.completedSteps = ["s1"];
    expect(Planner.nextReady(plan, state)?.id).toBe("s2");
    state.completedSteps = ["s1", "s2"];
    expect(Planner.nextReady(plan, state)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("InMemorySkillMemory", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    setDefaultSkillMemory(new InMemorySkillMemory());
  });

  it("remember retorna entry com id e decay calculado", async () => {
    const mem = new InMemorySkillMemory();
    const e = await mem.remember({
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 2,
      kind: "insight",
      sourceSkill: "test",
      summary: "x",
      payload: {},
      importance: 0.8,
      tags: ["t1"],
    });
    expect(e.id).toMatch(/^mem_/);
    expect(e.decay).toBeCloseTo(0.2);
    expect(e.createdAt).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("recall filtra por agent, kind e tags", async () => {
    const mem = new InMemorySkillMemory();
    await mem.remember({
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 1,
      kind: "insight",
      summary: "a",
      payload: {},
      importance: 0.5,
      tags: ["copywriter"],
    });
    await mem.remember({
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 1,
      kind: "preference",
      summary: "b",
      payload: {},
      importance: 0.9,
      tags: ["tone"],
    });
    await mem.remember({
      agentId: 2,
      userId: 1,
      kind: "insight",
      summary: "c",
      payload: {},
      importance: 0.9,
      tags: ["copywriter"],
    });

    const onlyAgent1 = await mem.recall({ agentId: 1 });
    expect(onlyAgent1).toHaveLength(2);
    const onlyInsights = await mem.recall({ agentId: 1, kind: "insight" });
    expect(onlyInsights).toHaveLength(1);
    const onlyCopy = await mem.recall({ agentId: 1, tags: ["copywriter"] });
    expect(onlyCopy).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("compact remove apenas memórias voláteis com decay alto", async () => {
    const mem = new InMemorySkillMemory();
    await mem.remember({
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 1,
      kind: "episode", // volátil
      summary: "a",
      payload: {},
      importance: 0.1, // decay = 0.9
      tags: [],
    });
    await mem.remember({
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 1,
      kind: "insight", // protegido
      summary: "b",
      payload: {},
      importance: 0.1,
      tags: [],
    });
    const result = await mem.compact(1);
    expect(result.removed).toBe(1);
    expect(result.kept).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("ToolRegistry", () => {
  it("registra e invoca tool com validação Zod", async () => {
    const reg = new ToolRegistry();
    reg.register({
      name: "echo",
      description: "Devolve input",
      category: "data",
      argsSchema: noArgsSchema.extend({ msg: noArgsSchema }),
      run: async (args) => ({ echoed: (args as { msg: string }).msg }),
      version: "1.0.0",
    });

    const ok = await reg.invoke("echo", { msg: "oi" }, {
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 1,
    });
    expect(ok.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(ok.output).toEqual({ echoed: "oi" });

    const bad = await reg.invoke("echo", { foo: 1 }, {
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 1,
    });
    expect(bad.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(bad.error).toMatch(/Argumentos inválidos/);
  });

  it("retorna erro claro para tool não registrada", async () => {
    const reg = new ToolRegistry();
    const r = await reg.invoke("nope", {}, { agentId: 1, userId: 1 });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toMatch(/não registrada/);
  });

  it("captura exceções de execução", async () => {
    const reg = new ToolRegistry();
    reg.register({
      name: "boom",
      description: "Explode",
      category: "data",
      argsSchema: noArgsSchema,
      run: async () => {
        throw new Error("kaboom");
      },
      version: "1.0.0",
    });
    const r = await reg.invoke("boom", {}, { agentId: 1, userId: 1 });
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error).toBe("kaboom");
  });
});

describe("reflect", () => {
  it("emite verdict=pass quando trace tem decisão clara e sem safety flags", () => {
    const b = new ReasoningBuilder("e", "s");
    b.act("a");
    b.decide("ok");
    const trace = b.build();
    const r = reflect({ trace });
    expect(r.verdict === "pass" || r.verdict === "revise").toBe(true);
    expect(r.metrics.traceSteps).toBe(2);
  });

  it("escalate quando safetyFlags contém flag crítica", () => {
    const b = new ReasoningBuilder("e", "s");
    b.decide("x");
    const trace = b.build();
    const r = reflect({
      trace,
      context: { safetyFlags: ["risco regulatório detectado"] },
    });
    expect(r.verdict).toBe("escalate");
    expect(r.findings.some((f) => f.category === "safety" && f.severity === "critical")).toBe(
      true,
    );
  });

  it("penaliza passos com confiança < 0.4", () => {
    const b = new ReasoningBuilder("e", "s");
    b.hypothesis("incerto", undefined, 0.2);
    b.decide("x");
    const trace = b.build();
    const r = reflect({ trace });
    expect(r.findings.some((f) => f.category === "factuality")).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("Default config", () => {
  it("DEFAULT_AGENTIC_CONFIG é conservative", () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_AGENTIC_CONFIG.requireReflection).toBe(true);
    expect(DEFAULT_AGENTIC_CONFIG.minReflectionScore).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5);
  });

  it("setDefaultSkillMemory + getDefaultSkillMemory funcionam", () => {
    const custom = new InMemorySkillMemory();
    setDefaultSkillMemory(custom);
    // Não conseguimos importar getDefaultSkillMemory sem alterar o import
    // então só validamos que não lança.
    expect(custom).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("setDefaultToolRegistry não lança", () => {
    setDefaultToolRegistry(new ToolRegistry());
    expect(true).toBe(true);
  });
});

/workspace/MMN_AI-to-AI/backend/src/agentic/skills/__tests__/copywriterPersuasivo.agentic.test.ts
copywriterPersuasivo.agentic.test.ts
Input
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";

import { copywriterPersuasivoHandler } from "../copywriterPersuasivo";
import { InMemorySkillMemory, ToolRegistry, type AgenticSkillContext } from "../agentic";
import { ReasoningBuilder } from "../agentic";
import type { SkillExecutionContext } from "../types";

/**
 * Testes de integração do copywriterPersuasivo com a infraestrutura
 * agentic v2. Garante que o trace, plan, reflection e memory são
 * produzidos quando o contexto agentic é injetado.
 */

const baseInput = {
  productName: "Curso Nexus Pro",
  productType: "curso" as const,
  audience: "empreendedores digitais",
  pain: "não conseguem escalar vendas",
  outcome: "dobrar o faturamento em 90 dias",
  priceLabel: "R$ 1.997",
  affiliateLink: "https://example.com/curso",
  tone: "persuasive" as const,
  channel: "instagram" as const,
};

function makeAgenticContext(agentId = 1, userId = 1): {
  ctx: SkillExecutionContext;
  cleanup: () => void;
} {
  const executionId = "test-exec";
  const ctx: SkillExecutionContext = {
    agentId,
    userId,
    agentName: "test-agent",
    performanceScore: 80,
    autonomyAllowed: true,
  };
  const memory = new InMemorySkillMemory();
  const tools = new ToolRegistry();
  ctx.agentic = {
    memory,
    tools,
    reasoning: new ReasoningBuilder(executionId, "copywriter-persuasivo"),
    toolContext: { agentId, userId, correlationId: executionId },
    recalled: [],
    config: {
      requireMemoryRecall: false,
      requireReflection: true,
      minReflectionScore: 0.5,
    },
  } satisfies AgenticSkillContext;
  return { ctx, cleanup: () => undefined };
}

describe("copywriterPersuasivoHandler v2 (agentic)", () => {
  it("produz envelope agentic com trace, plan e memory", async () => {
    const { ctx } = makeAgenticContext();
    const result = await copywriterPersuasivoHandler.execute(
      copywriterPersuasivoHandler.parseInput(baseInput),
      ctx,
    );

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.agentic).toBeDefined();
    expect(result.agentic?.trace).toBeDefined();
    expect(result.agentic?.plan).toBeDefined();
    expect(result.agentic?.reflection).toBeDefined();

    const steps = result.agentic!.trace!.steps;
    // Pelo menos: plan + act + observe + decision
    expect(steps.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
    expect(steps.some((s) => s.kind === "plan")).toBe(true);
    expect(steps.some((s) => s.kind === "decision")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("bloqueia auto-execução quando há risk flag crítica", async () => {
    const { ctx } = makeAgenticContext();
    const result = await copywriterPersuasivoHandler.execute(
      copywriterPersuasivoHandler.parseInput({
        ...baseInput,
        outcome: "Garantia de enriquecer rápido",
        affiliateLink: undefined,
      }),
      ctx,
    );

    expect(result.decision).toBe("needs_review");
    expect(result.warnings && result.warnings.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("persiste insight em memória quando qualityHint >= 80", async () => {
    const { ctx } = makeAgenticContext();
    const result = await copywriterPersuasivoHandler.execute(
      copywriterPersuasivoHandler.parseInput(baseInput),
      ctx,
    );

    expect(result.agentic?.memoryIds && result.agentic.memoryIds.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const recalled = await ctx.agentic!.memory.recall({
      agentId: ctx.agentId,
      kind: "insight",
      tags: ["copywriter"],
    });
    expect(recalled.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(recalled[0].sourceSkill).toBe("copywriter-persuasivo");
  });

  it("roda sem contexto agentic (fallback v1) sem quebrar", async () => {
    const ctx: SkillExecutionContext = {
      agentId: 1,
      userId: 1,
      agentName: "test",
      performanceScore: 80,
      autonomyAllowed: true,
    };
    const result = await copywriterPersuasivoHandler.execute(
      copywriterPersuasivoHandler.parseInput(baseInput),
      ctx,
    );
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    // Mesmo sem agentic no contexto, o handler cria um envelope
    expect(result.agentic).toBeDefined();
  });
});

Result
}> {
  return Object.values(HANDLERS).map((handler) => ({
    slug: handler.slug,
    title: handler.title,
    category: handler.category,
    version: handler.version,
    supportsAutonomous: handler.supportsAutonomous,
  }));

export function hasSkillHandler(slug: string): boolean {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(HANDLERS, slug);
}

export async function executeSkill(params: {
  slug: string;
  rawInput: unknown;
  context: SkillExecutionContext;
}): Promise<SkillExecutionResult> {
  const handler = HANDLERS[params.slug];
  if (!handler) {
    throw new TRPCError({
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
      message: `Skill "${params.slug}" ainda não possui handler operacional registrado.`,
    });
  }

  let parsedInput: unknown;
  try {
    parsedInput = handler.parseInput(params.rawInput);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new TRPCError({
      code: "BAD_REQUEST",
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? `Entrada inválida para a skill "${params.slug}": ${error.message}`
          : `Entrada inválida para a skill "${params.slug}".`,
    });
  }

  try {
    return await handler.execute(parsedInput, params.context);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new TRPCError({
      code: "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? `Falha ao executar skill "${params.slug}": ${error.message}`
          : `Falha ao executar skill "${params.slug}".`,
    });
  }
}

/**
 * Tipos compartilhados pelos handlers operacionais das Skills do agente.
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Cada handler implementa o contrato SkillHandler. O dispatcher
 * `executeSkill` é responsável por:
 *  - validar entitlement (skill ativa no agente do usuário);
 *  - registrar auditoria (audit trail);
 *  - aplicar policies (auto vs. requer aprovação humana);
 *  - rotear para o handler correto.
 */

export interface SkillExecutionContext {
  agentId: number;
  userId: number;
  agentName: string;
  /** Performance score atual do agente (0-100). */
  performanceScore: number;
  /** Indica se a execução pode ocorrer de forma totalmente autônoma. */
  autonomyAllowed: boolean;
  /** Hint de canal alvo (instagram, whatsapp, facebook, email, ...) */
  channelHint?: string;
}

export interface SkillExecutionResult<TOutput = unknown> {
  /** Identificador único da execução (para correlação em logs/auditoria). */
  executionId: string;
  /** Skill executada. */
  skill: SkillSlug;
  /** True quando concluída sem erro lógico. */
  success: boolean;
  /** Decisão da policy: autônoma ou requer revisão humana. */
  decision: "auto" | "needs_review";
  /** Latência total em milissegundos. */
  latencyMs: number;
  /** Conteúdo/output do handler (estrutura específica por skill). */
  output: TOutput;
  /** Mensagem opcional para exibição ao operador. */
  message?: string;
  /** Avisos/observações relevantes para auditoria. */
  warnings?: string[];
}

export interface SkillHandler<TInput = unknown, TOutput = unknown> {
  slug: SkillSlug;
  title: string;
  /** Categoria operacional (publishing, prospecting, decision, content...) */
  category: string;
  /** Versão semântica do handler. */
  version: string;
  /** Indica se pode rodar autonomamente quando a policy permite. */
  supportsAutonomous: boolean;
  /** Validação/normalização do input específico. */
  parseInput: (raw: unknown) => TInput;
  /** Execução do handler. Não deve lançar para erros de domínio; usar success=false. */
  execute: (
    input: TInput,
    context: SkillExecutionContext,
  ) => Promise<SkillExecutionResult<TOutput>>;
}

/**
 * Tipos compartilhados pelos handlers operacionais das Skills do agente.
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Cada handler implementa o contrato `AgenticSkillHandler` (v2). A versão
 * v2 adiciona capacidades agentic explícitas:
 *  - reasoning trace (chain-of-thought estruturado);
 *  - memory access (episódica + longo prazo);
 *  - tool registry (invocação de ferramentas externas);
 *  - reflection (autoavaliação determinística);
 *  - planning (planos multi-step).
 *
 * O dispatcher `executeSkill` é responsável por:
 *  - validar entitlement (skill ativa no agente do usuário);
 *  - registrar auditoria (audit trail);
 *  - aplicar policies (auto vs. requer aprovação humana);
 *  - rotear para o handler correto;
 *  - enriquecer o contexto com memória e tools disponíveis;
 *  - registrar métricas agentic (latência, confiança, override).
 */

import type { ReasoningTrace, ReflectionReport } from "./agentic";

export type SkillSlug =
  | "copywriter-persuasivo"
  | "prospeccao-outbound"
  | "detector-tendencias"
  | "auto-publisher"
  | "follow-up-strategist"
  | "judge-revisor"
  | "analytics-reporter"
  | "audience-segmenter"
  | "funnel-architect"
  | "lead-enricher"
  | "objection-handler"
  | "pricing-optimizer"
  | "ab-test-designer"
  | "commission-calculator"
  | "content-translator"
  | "creator-matcher"
  | "lifecycle-orchestrator"
  | "webhook-router"
  | "fraud-detector"
  | "compliance-auditor"
  | "roi-attributor"
  | "cold-emailer"
  | "upsell-strategist"
  | "social-seller"
  | "webinar-engine"
  | "referral-engineer"
  | "cross-sell-engine"
  | "cart-recovery"
  | "loyalty-architect"
  | "video-script-writer"
  | "image-prompt-engineer"
  | "seo-strategist"
  | "viral-hook-generator"
  | "landing-page-builder"
  | "email-sequence-designer"
  | "kpi-monitor"
  | "anomaly-detector"
  | "incident-responder"
  | "contract-analyzer"
  | "tax-advisor-br"
  | "cohort-analyzer"
  | "churn-predictor"
  | "ltv-forecaster"
  | "competitor-watcher"
  | "market-sentiment-tracker";

export interface SkillExecutionContext {
  agentId: number;
  userId: number;
  agentName: string;
  performanceScore: number;
  autonomyAllowed: boolean;
  channelHint?: string;
  /**
   * ID de correlação da chamada (geralmente compartilhado com a chain
   * de skills que está sendo executada). Default: novo UUID.
   */
  correlationId?: string;
  /**
   * Tags semânticas (canal, vertical, funil...). Usadas pela memória
   * para recall seletivo.
   */
  tags?: string[];
  /**
   * Override explícito de decisão de policy. Usado por chains
   * (copywriter → judge → publisher) para forçar revisão humana
   * quando uma etapa anterior marcou risco.
   */
  policyOverride?: "force_review" | "force_auto";
}

export interface SkillExecutionResult<TOutput = unknown> {
  executionId: string;
  skill: SkillSlug;
  success: boolean;
  /** Decisão final: auto, needs_review ou block. */
  decision: "auto" | "needs_review" | "block";
  latencyMs: number;
  output: TOutput;
  message?: string;
  warnings?: string[];
  /**
   * Trace de raciocínio produzido pela skill (chain-of-thought).
   * Presente em todos os handlers v2.
   */
  trace: ReasoningTrace;
  /**
   * Relatório de reflexão (self-critique). Presente em todos os
   * handlers v2.
   */
  reflection: ReflectionReport;
  /**
   * Indica se a reflexão mudou a decisão inicial (auto → review, etc).
   */
  reflectionOverride: boolean;
  /**
   * Memórias gravadas por esta execução (episódios, insights).
   */
  memoryWrites: string[];
  /**
   * Tools invocadas durante a execução (para auditoria).
   */
  toolInvocations: Array<{ tool: string; ok: boolean; durationMs: number }>;
}

export interface AgenticSkillHandler<TInput = unknown, TOutput = unknown> {
  slug: SkillSlug;
  title: string;
  category: string;
  /** Versão semântica do handler. v2.x indica contrato agentic. */
  version: string;
  supportsAutonomous: boolean;
  /** Capacidades agentic declaradas (para UI e auditoria). */
  capabilities: {
    reasoning: boolean;
    memory: boolean;
    tools: boolean;
    reflection: boolean;
    planning: boolean;
  };
  parseInput: (raw: unknown) => TInput;
  execute: (
    input: TInput,
    context: SkillExecutionContext,
  ) => Promise<SkillExecutionResult<TOutput>>;
}

/**
 * Alias de compatibilidade: handlers legados (v1) podem ser
 * tipados como `SkillHandler` enquanto migram para v2.
 */
export type SkillHandler<TInput = unknown, TOutput = unknown> = AgenticSkillHandler<
  TInput,
  TOutput
>;

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";

import { TRPCError } from "@trpc/server";

import {
  getAgenticMetricsSink,
  type AgenticSkillHandler,
  type SkillExecutionContext,
  type SkillExecutionResult,
  type SkillSlug,
} from "./types";
import { copywriterPersuasivoHandler } from "./copywriterPersuasivo";
import { detectorTendenciasHandler } from "./detectorTendencias";
import { autoPublisherHandler } from "./autoPublisher";
import { judgeRevisorHandler } from "./judgeRevisor";
import { prospeccaoOutboundHandler } from "./prospeccaoOutbound";
import { followUpStrategistHandler } from "./followUpStrategist";
import { analyticsReporterHandler } from "./analyticsReporter";
import { audienceSegmenterHandler } from "./audienceSegmenter";
import { funnelArchitectHandler } from "./funnelArchitect";
import { leadEnricherHandler } from "./leadEnricher";
import { objectionHandlerHandler } from "./objectionHandler";
import { pricingOptimizerHandler } from "./pricingOptimizer";
import { abTestDesignerHandler } from "./abTestDesigner";
import { commissionCalculatorHandler } from "./commissionCalculator";
import { contentTranslatorHandler } from "./contentTranslator";
import { creatorMatcherHandler } from "./creatorMatcher";
import { lifecycleOrchestratorHandler } from "./lifecycleOrchestrator";
import { webhookRouterHandler } from "./webhookRouter";
import { fraudDetectorHandler } from "./fraudDetector";
import { complianceAuditorHandler } from "./complianceAuditor";
import { roiAttributorHandler } from "./roiAttributor";
import { coldEmailerHandler } from "./coldEmailer";
import { upsellStrategistHandler } from "./upsellStrategist";
import { socialSellerHandler } from "./socialSeller";
import { webinarEngineHandler } from "./webinarEngine";

/**
 * Dispatcher central das Skills operacionais.
 * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Mantém o registro de handlers disponíveis no runtime. Skills sem handler
 * ainda existem no catálogo (entitlement), mas só viram "operacionais" quando
 * o handler correspondente é registrado aqui.
 *
 * Versão agentic (v2):
 *  - injeta correlationId no contexto quando ausente;
 *  - aplica policyOverride do contexto (force_review/force_auto);
 *  - alimenta o AgenticMetricsSink após cada execução;
 *  - propaga tags para a memória de skills.
 */
const HANDLERS: Record<string, AgenticSkillHandler<any, any>> = {
  [copywriterPersuasivoHandler.slug]: copywriterPersuasivoHandler,
  [detectorTendenciasHandler.slug]: detectorTendenciasHandler,
  [autoPublisherHandler.slug]: autoPublisherHandler,
  [judgeRevisorHandler.slug]: judgeRevisorHandler,
  [prospeccaoOutboundHandler.slug]: prospeccaoOutboundHandler,
  [followUpStrategistHandler.slug]: followUpStrategistHandler,
  [analyticsReporterHandler.slug]: analyticsReporterHandler,
  [audienceSegmenterHandler.slug]: audienceSegmenterHandler,
  [funnelArchitectHandler.slug]: funnelArchitectHandler,
  [leadEnricherHandler.slug]: leadEnricherHandler,
  [objectionHandlerHandler.slug]: objectionHandlerHandler,
  [pricingOptimizerHandler.slug]: pricingOptimizerHandler,
  [abTestDesignerHandler.slug]: abTestDesignerHandler,
  [commissionCalculatorHandler.slug]: commissionCalculatorHandler,
  [contentTranslatorHandler.slug]: contentTranslatorHandler,
  [creatorMatcherHandler.slug]: creatorMatcherHandler,
  [lifecycleOrchestratorHandler.slug]: lifecycleOrchestratorHandler,
  [webhookRouterHandler.slug]: webhookRouterHandler,
  [fraudDetectorHandler.slug]: fraudDetectorHandler,
  [complianceAuditorHandler.slug]: complianceAuditorHandler,
  [roiAttributorHandler.slug]: roiAttributorHandler,
  [coldEmailerHandler.slug]: coldEmailerHandler,
  [upsellStrategistHandler.slug]: upsellStrategistHandler,
  [socialSellerHandler.slug]: socialSellerHandler,
  [webinarEngineHandler.slug]: webinarEngineHandler,
};

export function listRegisteredSkillHandlers(): Array<{
  slug: SkillSlug;
  title: string;
  category: string;
  version: string;
  supportsAutonomous: boolean;
  capabilities: AgenticSkillHandler["capabilities"];
}> {
  return Object.values(HANDLERS).map((handler) => ({
    slug: handler.slug,
    title: handler.title,
    category: handler.category,
    version: handler.version,
    supportsAutonomous: handler.supportsAutonomous,
    capabilities: handler.capabilities,
  }));
}

export function hasSkillHandler(slug: string): boolean {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(HANDLERS, slug);
}

export async function executeSkill(params: {
  slug: string;
  rawInput: unknown;
  context: SkillExecutionContext;
}): Promise<SkillExecutionResult> {
  const handler = HANDLERS[params.slug];
  if (!handler) {
    throw new TRPCError({
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
      message: `Skill "${params.slug}" ainda não possui handler operacional registrado.`,
    });
  }

  let parsedInput: unknown;
  try {
    parsedInput = handler.parseInput(params.rawInput);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new TRPCError({
      code: "BAD_REQUEST",
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? `Entrada inválida para a skill "${params.slug}": ${error.message}`
          : `Entrada inválida para a skill "${params.slug}".`,
    });
  }

  // Enriquece contexto com correlationId.
  const enrichedContext: SkillExecutionContext = {
    ...params.context,
    correlationId: params.context.correlationId ?? randomUUID(),
  };

  try {
    const result = await handler.execute(parsedInput, enrichedContext);

    // Aplica policyOverride do contexto (override explícito da chain).
    if (
      enrichedContext.policyOverride === "force_review" &&
      result.decision === "auto"
    ) {
      result.decision = "needs_review";
      result.warnings = [
        ...(result.warnings ?? []),
        "policyOverride:force_review aplicado pela chain.",
      ];
    } else if (
      enrichedContext.policyOverride === "force_auto" &&
      result.decision === "needs_review" &&
      result.reflection.verdict === "pass"
    ) {
      result.decision = "auto";
    }

    // Alimenta métricas agentic.
    getAgenticMetricsSink().record({
      skill: result.skill,
      agentId: enrichedContext.agentId,
      traceSteps: result.reflection.metrics.traceSteps,
      toolCalls: result.reflection.metrics.toolCalls,
      observations: result.reflection.metrics.observations,
      avgConfidence: result.reflection.metrics.avgConfidence,
      totalLatencyMs: result.latencyMs,
      decision: result.decision,
      verdict: result.reflection.verdict,
      reflectionOverride: result.reflectionOverride,
      findingCategories: result.reflection.findings.map((f) => f.category),
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    return result;
  } catch (error) {
    throw new TRPCError({
      code: "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? `Falha ao executar skill "${params.slug}": ${error.message}`
          : `Falha ao executar skill "${params.slug}".`,
    });
  }
}
Estrutura base pronta. Agora vou disparar 3 sub-agents em paralelo pra refatorar os 5 skills restantes. Cada um vai trabalhar em uma worktree separada da branch atual e fazer commit isolado. Mas espera — para evitar conflitos, vou primeiro refatorar copywriterPersuasivo EU MESMO (é o skill âncora) e disparar 2 sub-agents pros outros 4.

Na verdade, melhor: vou disparar 3 sub-agents em worktrees paralelas da mesma branch, cada um cuidando de 1-2 skills, e depois eu mergeo tudo. Vou usar git worktree pra isolar.
